urai:- araica - aracē, niṉ akattu māṭciyatu - niṉ maṉattiṉ māṇpāṉatu, peritu aḻakiyatāyiṟṟu - mikavum aḻakākavuḷatu, uraiceytāl - yām em muṉṉaip piṟavi varalāṟṟaic colla luṟṟāl, uṟutiyāyatu - niṉakku uṟutiyākum poruḷiṉai, uṇarntu - tērntaṟintu, koṇṭu - ataṉaiyē mēṟkoṇṭu, uytipōlum - uytipeṟuvāyāka, virai ceytār - maṇam kamaḻum mālaiyaṇinta; varai cey mārpa - malaipōlum mārpaiyuṭaiyōy, viṉaviya poruḷ itu ellām - nī kēṭṭa ipporuḷ aṉaittaiyum, yāṉ niraiceytu pukalvaṉ - yāṉ varicaippaṭutti muṟaiyāka vuraippēṉ, niṉaivoṭu - oru mukamāṉa karuttuṭaṉ, itukēḷ - itaṉaik kēṭpāyāka; eṉṟāṉ - eṉṟu apayaruci kūṟiṉāṉ e-ṟu.
araica. moḻiyiṭaippōli. “iṉṉiṉi iṟaivaṉ nīyē eṉakku” (67) eṉṟu vāyāṟ colli, meyyāl “iṟaiñci niṉṟu,” muṉṉaip “pavattoṭu parivum” moḻika eṉṟu vēṇṭiyatu kaṇṭu, apayaruci maṉam kaṉintu aracaṉ kēṭṭatu kūṟaluṟukiṉṟāṉāka, avvaracaṉatu maṉavēṭkaiyiṉ mikuti avaṉ mukattē tikaḻakkaṇṭu pārāṭṭuvāṉāy, “niṉ akattu māṭciyatu peritu aḻakitāyiṟṟu” eṉṟum, muṉṉaip pavamum parivum moḻintu celluṅkāl iṭaiyiṭaiyē uṟutiyāya poruḷkaḷ viravivarumātaliṉ, avaṟṟai aṟivāl tērntu mēṟkoṇṭu uytal vēṇṭum eṉpāṉ, “uṟutiyāya tuṇarntu koṇṭuyti”  eṉṟum kūṟiṉāṉ. pōlum, uraiyacai; itaṉai vaṭanūlār vākkiyālaṅkāram eṉpa. viraiceytār varai ceymārpa eṉpatu iṇai
etukai tārmārpa, varaicey mārpa eṉa iyaiyum. itaṟkap piṟavāṟum kūṟupa. nirai ceyturaiyāvaḻi, iṭaiyiṭaiyē viravappaṭum uṟutip poruḷkaḷ iṉitu tērntu koḷḷap paṭāvātaliṉ, “nirai ceytē pukalvaṉ” eṉṟāṉ, ēkāram, tēṟṟam. uṟutiyāya kēḷviyāl tōṭṭa ceviyaṉallaṉātal tōṉṟa, “niṉaivoṭu kēḷ” eṉa vaṟpuṟuttiṉāṉ.
kēḷviyālām payaṉ kūṟal
70.	evvaḷa vitaṉaik kēṭpā riruviṉai kaḻuvu nīrār
	avvaḷa vavaruk kūṟṟuc ceṟittuṭa ṉutirppai yākkum
	meyvakai terintu māṟṟai veruviṉar vīṭṭai yeytuñ
	cevviya rākac ceytu ciṟappiṉai niṟuttum vēntē.
urai:- vēntē - aracē, itaṉai evvaḷavu kēṭpār - yām kūṟum ivvaṟavuraiyai oruvar evvaḷavu kēṭkiṉṟārō, iruviṉai kaḻuvum nīrār - avar iruvakai viṉaikaḷākiya aḻukkiṉaik kaḻuvikkoḷḷum taṉmaiyuṭaiyarāvar, avvaḷavu - avvaḷavum, avarukku ūṟṟu ceṟittu - avarukku uṟukiṉṟaṉa vākiya - ūṟṟukaḷaiyum aṭaittu, utirppai uṭaṉ ākkum - naṉ ñāṉattaip peṟuvikkum, meyvakai terintu - uyir mutaliya tattuvak kūṟukaḷai yārāyntu, māṟṟai veruviṉar vīṭṭai - ulaka vāḻkkaiyai yañcit tuṟavupūṇṭō reytum vīṭu pēṟṟiṉai, eytum - eytak kūṭiya, cevviyarākac ceytu -  takutiyuṭaiyarākki, ciṟappiṉai niṟuttum - mutticcīvaṉākiya ciṟappai nilaipeṟuvikkum e-ṟu.
icceyyukaḷāl, ivvaṟaṅkēṭṭalāl varum payaṉ kūṟukiṉṟāṉātaliṉ, “eṉaittāṉum nallavai kēṭka aṉait tāṉum āṉṟa perumai tarum”1 eṉṟa karuttaiyuṭkoṇṭu, mutaṟkaṇ ikkēḷvi iruviṉaikaḷākiya aḻukkaik kaḻuvit tūymai ceytu koḷḷum takutiyai yuṇṭupaṇṇum eṉṟaṟku, “kēṭpār iruviṉai kaḻuvum nīrār” eṉṟāṉ. ūṟṟu - iruviṉaiyum uyiraiyuṟuvatu; uṟuvatu, ūṟṟu - irumpaip paḻukkak kāycciya viṭattu ataṉiṭattē uṟṟuttōṉṟum nīrpōla uyiriṭattē iruviṉaiyum tōṉṟutalāl ūṟṟāyiṟ ṟeṉpar, “ōtiya viraṇṭum (nalviṉai  tīviṉaikaḷ) uyiriṉai yuṟutal, ūṟṟām, tātuṟak kāynta pōḻtil tāṉuṟum nīrai yottē”2 eṉṟu cāṉṟōr kūṟutal kāṇka. iḵtu īṉam, atikam, īrāpatakam, cāmparāyam, ñāṉam, aññāṉam, puṇṇiyam, pāvam, taviyam, pariṇāmam eṉap pattu vakaiyākum. ivvūṟṟukkaḷ tavattāl aṭaikkap paṭuvaṉavām; “ūṉantīrtavattiṉūṟṟuc ceṟitta mātavaṉai yētti”3 eṉa varutal kāṇka. aṭaittalāvatu kupti, camiti, tarumam, cintai, aṭakkam, tāpaṉam eṉpaṉavaṟṟāl “paricai vellum taṉmai”4 eṉpar. ivaṟṟuḷ aṭakkam, tāpaṉam, paricai veṟṟi eṉṟa mūṉṟum, naṉṉiṉaivu, nalluṇarvu iraṇṭumkūṭi mutti nilaiyai yuṇṭupaṇṇum eṉpavākaliṉ, “ūṟṟuc ceṟittuṭaṉ utirppai yākkum” eṉṟāṉ. aṭaippu, ceṟippeṉap paṭutaliṉ, ūṟṟuc ceṟippu utirppai yākkum eṉṟāṉāyiṟṟu, “niṉṟa vantattiṉ mūṉṟum. (aṭakkam mutaliya mūṉṟum) niṉaippu uṇarvu utirppai yākkum” eṉṟu piṟarum5 kūṟutal kāṇka. ivaṟṟiṉ virivellām patārtta cāram mērumantarapurāṇam mutaliya nūlkaḷuṭ kaṇṭu koḷka. ulaka vāḻviṉ poymmaiyum mutti vāḻviṉ meymmaiyum vēṟupaṭuttu nōkkumiṭattu, uṭalōṭu kūṭic ceyyum ulaka vāḻvu tuṉpa ētuvāvatu tōṉṟutaliṉ, aṟivuṭaiyōr mutti vāḻvait terintuṇarntu, ulaka vāḻviṟku añcit tuṟavu pūṇṭu vīṭṭu vāḻvai urimai ceytukoḷvar eṉṟaṟku, “meyvakai terintu māṟṟai veruviṉarvīṭṭai” yeṉṟāṉ. meyvakai teriyātār ulaka vāḻvil tuṉpamum, terintār vīṭṭu vāḻvil iṉpamum nukarvar eṉṟa karuttēpaṭa, “maṉṟalar muṭiyiṉāy māṟṟum vīṭṭu mām, ceṉṟa tattuvanteḷiyāmai tēṟalāl”1 eṉṟu vāmaṉa muṉikaḷum  uraittal kāṇka. mey - tattuvam. vīṭṭiṉpa vāḻvai nōkka, ulaka vāḻvu tuṉpamāy māṟupaṭṭiruttaliṉ “māṟṟu” eṉṟu camaṇ cāṉṟōr kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. vīṭṭuyiriṉ ciṟappāvatu, “vevviṉaiyeṇmaiyiṉ, kēṭṭil eṇkuṇa meytiyōr kēṭilā, māṭciyāl”2 ulakantoḻa viḷaṅkuvatu. kēṭpataṉāl uḷatām payaṉ teriyāvaḻik kēṭpōrkkuk kēḷvikkaṇ maṉam cellātu eṉṉum karuttuppaṟṟi ivvaḷavum apayaruci tokuttuk kūṟiṉāṉeṉa vuṇarka.
ituvumatu
71.	malamali kurampai yiṉkaṇ maṉateḻu vikaṟpai māṟṟum
	pulamali pōkat tiṉkaṇ ṇācaiyaip poṉṟu vikkum
	kolaimali koṭumai taṉṉaik kuṟaittiṭu maṉattiṟ kōlac
	cilaimali nutali ṉārtaṅ kātalil tīmai ceyyum.
urai:- malam mali kurampaiyiṉkaṇ - (mēlē kūṟiya kēḷvi) malam niṟainta uṭampiṉkaṇṇatākiya, maṉattu eḻu vikaṟpai māṟṟum - maṉattil eḻukiṉṟa tiripuṇarccikaḷaip pōkki uṇmaiyuṇarvu kaḷākkum, pulam malipōkattiṉkaṇ - aimpulaṉkaḷālum nukarappaṭum nukarccikkaṇ uṇṭākum, ācaiyaip poṉṟuvikkum - ācaiyaik keṭuttu vīṭṭiṉ pattil vēṭkai nikaḻap paṇṇum, kolai mali koṭumai taṉṉaik kuṟaittiṭum - kolai mutaliya tīviṉaik kētuvākiya tīyavuṇarccikaḷait taḻaiyāvāṟu veṭṭi vīḻttum, kōlac cilaimali nutaliṉār tam kātaliṉ - aḻakiya vilpōlum puruvat tiṉaiyuṭaiya makaḷirpāl uṇṭākum kāmaviccaikku, maṉattil tīmai ceyyum - maṉattiṭattē koṭiya veṟuppiṉai yuṇṭu paṇṇum e-ṟu.
malam, aḻukku, kurampai, uṭampu; “pollāp puḻumali nōy puṉ kurampaip”1 eṉṟu oḷavaiyāruṅ kūṟutal kāṇka. ivvuṭampai iṭamākak koṇṭa maṉam, poruḷ, iṭam, kālam, pāvam, pavam, uruvam, peyar eṉṟa ivaṟṟāl tiripuṇarcci koḷvatātaliṉ, avvuṇarcci ivvaṟavuṇarvāl cemmai yeytum eṉṟaṟku, “maṉatteḻu vikaṟpai māṟṟum” eṉṟāṉ. pōkattiṉkaṭ piṟakkum ācai oṉṟu palavāyp perukip piṟavik kētuvā taliṉ, “acaiyaip poṉṟuvikkum” eṉṟāṉ. vikaṟpavuṇarvum, pōkattiṉkaṇ ācaiyum kolai mutaliya peruntīviṉaiyākiya marattai vaḷarttu mēlum pala viṉaikaḷākiya kaṉivakaikaḷai viḷaivittal paṟṟi, “kuṟaittiṭum” eṉṟāṉ. makaḷir pāl uṇṭākum kāma viccai maṉattiṉkaṇṇatākaliṉ, avviṭattē ēḻātavāṟu keṭuttaḻikkum eṉṟaṟkut “tīmai ceyyum” eṉṟāṉ. nutal - ākupeyar. neṟṟikkē yuraippiṉumām.
ituvumatu
72.	piṟantavar muyaṟci yālē peṟupaya ṉaṭaiva rallāl*
	iṟantaṉar piṟanta tillai yiruviṉai tāṉu milleṉ
	ṟaṟaintaṉa †raṟivil lāmai yatuviṭut taṟane ṟikkaṇ
	ciṟantaṉa muyalap paṇṇum ceppumip poruṇmai yeṉṟāṉ.
urai:- piṟantavar - maṇṇil uṭampoṭu piṟantavarkaḷ, peṟupayaṉ - peṟutaṟkuriya nalaṅkaḷai, muyaṟciyāl - tattam muyaṟciyiṉāl, aṭaivar eṉṟu - peṟuvārkaḷ eṉṟum, allāl - allāmalum, iṟantaṉar piṟantatillai eṉṟu - iṟantavarkaḷ piṟantatu kiṭaiyāteṉṟum, iruviṉaitāṉum il eṉṟu - iruviṉai eṉpavaikaḷum illaiyeṉṟum, aṟaintaṉar - colpavarkaḷ, aṟivillāmai atu viṭuttu - avvāṟu tām colvataṟkētuvākiya aṟiyāmaiyaip pōkki, aṟaneṟikkaṇ - aṟaneṟiyiṉ kaṇṇē celutti, ciṟantaṉa - vīṭupēṟṟuk kuriya vaṟṟaik kuṟittu, ceppum ipporuṇmai - yām ippōtu collukiṉṟatākiya ivvaṟap poruḷ, muyalappaṇṇum - muyaṟci ceyvikkum e-ṟu.
ipporuṇmai, aṟivillāmai viṭuttu, aṟaneṟikkaṇ celutti, ciṟantaṉa muyalappaṇṇum eṉṟu muṭikka. celutti, yeṉpatu eñci niṉṟatu. ipporuḷiṉ taṉmai itu veṉpāṉ, poruṇmaiyeṉ ṟoḻintāṉ. aṟaintavar tāmē aṟiyāmai viṭuttu aṟaneṟikkaṇ niṉṟu ciṟantaṉa muyalumāṟu ceyyu meṉṟumām. viṉaittoṭar peṉpa toṉṟu vēṇṭā, piṟantavar peṟum payaṉukku avar muyaṟciyē kāraṇa meṉpār, “muyaṟciyālē peṟupayaṉaṭaiva” reṉkiṉṟaṉar. maṟupiṟappillai, iruviṉaiyillai eṉpaṉa mutalāyiṉa  piṟar kūṟuvataik koṇṭu kūṟiyaṉavām. eṉṟu eṉpatu eṅkum kūṭṭappaṭṭatu. “viṉaipakai yeṉṟiraṇṭiṉeccam”1 eṉpuḻippōla.
tāṉ aṟamuṇarnta varalāṟu kūṟal
73.	aṟapporuḷ viḷaikkuṅ kāṭci yaruntava raruḷiṟ ṟaṉṟip
	piṟappuṇarn tataṉiṉ yāmē peyarttuṇarn tiṭavum paṭṭa
	tiṟappavu mataṉkaṇ tēṟṟam iṉituvait tiṭumi ṉeṉṟāṉ
	uṟappaṇin tevaru muḷḷan tuvantaṉar kēṭka luṟṟār.
urai:- aṟapporuḷ viḷaikkum kāṭci - yāṉ kūṟum aṟamākiya poruḷ viḷaivikkum payaṉai, aruntavar aruḷiṟṟaṉṟi - ariya tavattaiyuṭaiya muṉivararkaḷ urait taruḷiyataṉālumaṉṟi, piṟappuṇarntataṉiṉ - paḻam piṟappu varalāṟukaḷai yaṟiyu maṟivu ṭaimaiyāl, yāmē - emmāl peyarttum uṇarntiṭappaṭṭatu - maṟupaṭiyum uṇarappaṭṭa tākum, ataṉkaṇ  - ataṉāl avvaṟap poruḷiṉkaṇ, tēṟṟam - karuttai, iṟappavum - mikavum teḷivāka, iṉitu vaittiṭumiṉ - iṉitu vaittuk kēṭpīrāka, eṉṟāṉ - eṉṟu apayaruci kūṟiṉāṉāka, evarum - āṅkirunta yāvarum, uṟappaṇintu - mikavum paṇintu, uvantaṉar kēṭkaluṟṟār - viruppattuṭaṉ kēṭpārāyiṉar e-ṟu.
aṟamākiya poruḷāl viḷaiyum payaṉ karuvikaḷāl kāṇappaṭutaliṉ kāṭci yeṉappaṭṭatu. kōḻiyāyirunta kālattu akampaṉa muṉikaḷum, iraṭṭaiyarāyp piṟantapiṉ cutatta muṉikaḷum paḻampiṟappiṉai yuṇarttiṉamaiyiṉ, “aruntavar” eṉṟāṉ. tiruvaṟam uṇarntu tiruntiya aṟiviṉa rāṉapōtu tāmākavum uṇarntu koṇṭatu eṉpāṉ, “peyarttuṇarntiṭavum paṭṭatu” eṉṟāṉ. ummai pirittuk kūṭṭappaṭṭatu. iṟappavum iṉitu eṉpaṉa vaṉpuṟai kuṟittu niṉṟaṉa. ivvāṟu upatēca mukattālum kāṭci vakaiyālum uṇarntatu eṉavē, yāvarkkum apporuḷaik kēṭṭaṟku nāṭṭa muṇṭāyiṟṟeṉpār, “uṟappaṇintu evarum uḷḷat tuvantaṉar kēṭkaluṟṟār” eṉṟār. uvantaṉar, muṟṟeccam. aracaṉ mutal āṅkirunta makkaḷaṉaivarum eṉṟaṟku “evarum” eṉṟār.
mutaṟ carukkam muṭintatu.

iraṇṭāvatu carukkam
ippakutikkaṇ, avantināṭṭu uñcayiṉi nakarattē acōkaṉ eṉṉum vēntaṉ cantiramatiyeṉpāḷai maṇantu yacōtaraṉeṉṉum makaṉaippeṟṟu iṉitiruppatum, avaṉ orukāl taṉakku naraiyuṇṭāṉatu kaṇṭu varunti, yacōtaraṉai aracaṉākkiviṭṭut tavamēṟ koḷvāṉ kuṇataraṉeṉṉum muṉivaṉpāl aṟaṅkēṭṭut tavam purintiruppatum, yacōtaraṉ, aṟattai nekiḻttup poruḷiṉpaṅkaḷil tōyntu kiṭappatum, avaṟkum avaṉ maṉaivi amirtamatiyeṉpāṭkum yacōmatiyeṉṉum makaṉ piṟantiruppatum, cila yāṇṭukaṭkuppiṉ orunāḷ amirtamati kaṇavaṉōṭu paḷḷiyil kaṇṇuṟaṅkupavaḷ, aṭṭapaṅkaṉeṉṉum yāṉaippākaṉoruvaṉ māḷavapañcamam eṉṉum paṇṇaip pāṭakkēṭṭu maṉamuruki avaṉ pāṟ kaḷḷak kāmaṅ koḷvatum, kuṇavati eṉṉum tōḻi palavāṟu eṭuttōti avaḷ ceyalai vilakkavum, amirtamati ēlātu, aṭṭapaṅkaṉaik kūṭṭuvikka vēṇṭumeṉa akkuṇavatiyai vaṟpuṟuttuvatum, avaḷ icaintu ceṉṟu aṭṭapaṅkaṉai matiyuṭam paṭuppatum, amirtamatiyum aṭṭapaṅkaṉum kaḷḷakkāma nukarcci peṟṟuvaruvatum, avaṭku yacōtaraṉpāl muṉivu muṟukuvatum, aracaṉ aiyuṟṟu avaḷatu kaḷḷavoḻukkattai yaṟintu mutaṟkaṇ kolai puriya niṉaintu piṉ māṟuvatum, aracavaikkaṇ amirta matiyuṭaṉ acatiyāṭik kaṟippuraiyāl ikaḻvatum, taṉakkuḷḷē makaḷirpōkamum araciyaṟpōkamum veṟuttu, amirtamatiyiṉ kaḷḷavoḻukkattai oru kaṉaviṉmēl vaittut tāyākiya cantira matikku uraippatum, avaḷ caṇṭamārikku uyirppali tantu vaḻi paṭṭuk kaṉavukāṭṭiya tīṅku oḻika eṉpatum, avaṉ uṭaṉpaṭā toḻivatum, avaḷ ciṉantu māviṉāl kōḻiyoṉṟu ceytu paliyiṭṭuvaḻi paṭukaveṉpatum, avaṉ avvāṟē ceytapōtu mākkōḻi uyiruṭaiyatupōl kūvi viḻutalum, avaṉ varuntit tuṟavu pūṇpatum, amirtamati avaṉukkum cantiramatikkum nañcuṇavu tantu kolvatum, iruvarum vilaṅkukaḷāyp piṟappatum, yacōmati  aracaṉātalum piṟavum kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
avantināṭṭu uñcayiṉi nakaram
74.vaḷavayal vāriyiṉ malinta palpati
	aḷavaṟu caṉapata mavanti yāmatiṉ
	viḷaipaya ṉamararum virumpunīr maiya
	tuḷatoru nakaratuñ cayiṉi yeṉpavē.
urai:- vaḷavayal vāriyiṉ - vaḷaviya vayalkaḷākiya varuvāyāl, malinta - celvamniṟainta, palpati - palanakaraṅkaṭku iṭamākiya, avaḷaṟu caṉa patam - aḷavillāta makkaḷaiyuṭai nāṭu, avantiyām - avantināṭākum, atiṉviḷaipayaṉ - ataṉkaṇ vāḻvārk keytum pōkam, amararum virumpum nīrmaiyatu - tēvarum virumpat takka taṉmaittākum, oru nakar uḷatu - annāṭṭiṟkut talaiyāya nakar oṉṟu uṇṭu, atu uñcayiṉi eṉpa - ataṉai uñcayiṉi eṉṟu periyōr kūṟuvārkaḷ e-ṟu.
vayalvaḷattāl mikka viḷaiporuḷum, ataṉ vāyilākap peruñcelvamum perukutaliṉ, vaḷavayalai “vāri” yeṉṟār. vāri, varuvāy; “puyaleṉṉum vāri” (14) eṉṟa tirukkuṟaṭkup parimēlaḻakiyār kūṟum urai kāṇka. caṉapatam, nāṭu. ummai, ciṟappu. aḵteṉṉal vēṇṭum. āytam - vikārattāl tokkatu. uñcayiṉi, vaṭacoṟcitaivu. eṉpa veṉṟataṉai acaiyāk kiṉumamaiyum.
nāṭṭaracaṉ acōkaṉ eṉal
75.kantaṭu kaḷimata yāṉai maṉṉavaṉ
	intira ṉeṉuntiṟa lacōka ṉeṉṟuḷaṉ
	cantira matiyeṉum maṭantai taṉṉuṭaṉ
	antami luvakaiya ṉamarntu vaikunāḷ.
urai:- kantuaṭu kaḷimata yāṉai - kaṭṭuttaṟiyai alaikkum matakkaḷip piṉaiyuṭaiya yāṉaikaḷaiyuṭaiya, maṉṉavaṉ - aracaṉā vāṉ, intiraṉ eṉum tiṟal - tuṟakkavēntaṉāṉa intiraṉēyeṉṟu collattakka tiṟalpaṭaitta, acōkaṉ eṉṟu uḷaṉ - acōkaṉ eṉṟu peyar kūṟappaṭuvāṉ uḷaṉ, cantiramati eṉum maṭantai taṉṉuṭaṉ - cantiramatiyeṉṉum maṅkainallāḷuṭaṉ kūṭi, antam iluvakaiyil - muṭivillāta iṉpattaiyuṭaiyaṉāy, amarntu vaikumnāḷ - ataṉai virumpivāḻum nāṭkaḷil e-ṟu.
vaṟitēyuḷḷa yāṉai kantiṉai alaippataṟkuk kāraṇam matakkaḷippuṭaimai yeṉṟaṟku, “kāntaṭu kaḷimatayāṉai” eṉṟār. poḻikiṉṟa matamuṭaimai tōṉṟaṟku, “kaḷimatayāṉai” eṉal vēṇṭiṟṟu. pōkappēṟṟālēyaṉṟi valiyālum intiraṉai yoppāṉ eṉpār, “intiraṉeṉun tiṟal acōkaṉ” eṉṟār. maṭantai - paruvattālum potuviyalpālum kūṟiyatu. cantiramatiyiṉ kūṭṭam kūṭuntōṟum puttiṉpam payantuvantamaiyiṉ, “antamil uvakaiyaṉ” eṉṟār. antamiṉmai amarntu vaikaṟku ētu.
yacōtaraṉ piṟappu
76.intuvō riḷampiṟai payanta teṉṉavē
	cantira matiyoru taṉayaṉ tantaṉaḷ
	entuyar kaḷaipava ṉicō taraṉṉeṉa
	nantiya pukaḻavaṉ nāma mōtiṉāṉ.
urai:- intu - muḻumatiyāṉatu, ōr iḷampiṟai payanta teṉṉa. ōr piṟaiccantiraṉaip peṟṟāṟpōla, cantiramati - cantira matiyākiya araci, oru taṉayaṉ tantaṉaḷ - oru makaṉaip peṟṟāḷ, em tuyar kaḷaipavaṉ - eṅkaḷ tuṉpattait tuṭaippavaṉāṉa acōkamaṉṉaṉ, nantiya pukaḻa avaṉ - mikkapukaḻaiyuṭaiya anta makaṉukku, icōtaraṉ eṉa- yacōtaraṉeṉṟu, nāmam ōtiṉāṉ - peyar vaḻaṅkiṉāṉ e-ṟu.
 cantiramati yacōtaraṉaippeṟṟatu, muḻumati iḷampiṟaiyai īṉṟatupōla eṉṟār, cantiramatiyiṉ aṟivē yacōtaraṉ matiyait taṉvaḻi yoḻukuvitta tātaliṉ. muḻumati iḷamatiyaiyīṉal - ilporuḷuvamai. emmaippōl piṟavittuyarkaḷaiyum nalloḻukka muṭaiyaṉeṉpāṉ, “emtuyar kaḷaipava” eṉṟu apayaruci kūṟukiṉṟāṉ. em tuyar kaḷaipavaṉ eṉṟatai yacōtaraṟkē yēṟṟi, tuyarkaḷai pavaṉātalāl yacōtaraṉeṉap peyariṭṭāṉ eṉṟumām.
yacōtaraṉ iḷavaracaṉāy amirtamatiyeṉpāḷai
 maṇantu makiḻtal
77.iḷaṅkaḷiṟ ṟuḻuvaiyi* ṉēta miṉṟiyē
	vaḷaṅkeḻu kumaraṉum vaḷarntu maṉṉaṉāy
	viḷaṅkiḻai yamiḻtamuṉ matiyai vēḷviyāṉ
	uḷaṅkoḷap puṇarntuṭa ṉuvakai yeytiṉāṉ.
urai:- iḷaṅkaḷiṟṟu uḻuvaiyiṉ - āṇpulikkuruḷai pōla, ētam iṉṟi - nalivu ciṟitum iṉṟi, vaḷamkeḻu kumaraṉum - valiporuntiya yacōtaraṉum, vaḷarntu - vaḷarccipeṟṟu, maṉṉaṉāy - iḷavaracaṉāki, viḷaṅkuiḻai amiḻtamuṉmatiyai - viḷaṅkukiṉṟa aṇikalaṉkaḷai yuṭaiya amirtamatiyeṉpavaḷai, vēḷviyāṉ - tirumaṇattāl, uḷaṅkoḷa - maṉam iṉpam niṟaiya, puṇarntu - kūṭi, uṭaṉ uvakai eytiṉāṉ - uṭaṉeytum iṉpattai nukarntu varalāṉāṉ e-ṟu.
kaḷiṟu - vilaṅkiṉ āṇpāṟpeyar; “ēṟum ēṟṟaiyum oruttalum kaḷiṟum”1 eṉa āciriyar kūṟutal kāṇka. ik kaḷiṟeṉṉuñ col vēḻam,kēḻal eṉṟa iraṇṭaṟkum ciṟappaveytuvippavāyiṉum īṇṭu itu potuvitiyāṉ amaintateṉa vuṇarka. “iḷaṅkaḷi ṟuḻuvaiyiṉ ētamiṉṟiyē” eṉṟu pāṭaṅ
koḷḷiṉ, puliyāl tīṅkiṉṟi iḷaṅkaḷiṟu vaḷarntāṟpōla eṉṟu poruḷ koḷka. acōkaṉāṭciyilātal, yacōtaraṉāṭciyilātal uḻuvai pōlum pakaiyuṇmai kūṟappaṭāmaiyiṉ, atu pāṭa maṉmaiyaṟika. vaḷam - īṇṭu meyvaṉmai mēṟṟu. “maṉṉaṉāy” eṉṟu īṇṭukkūṟiyatu, piṟāṇṭu, “yacōtaraṉ ṉeṉuntaṉayaṉai nilamakaṭṭalaivaṉā keṉa”(83)  muṭicūṭṭal kūṟappaṭutaliṉ, iḷavara caṉātalaikkuṟittu niṟpatāyiṟṟu. puṇarcci nikaḻumiṭattē iṉpamum uṭaṉnikaḻtalpaṟṟi, “uṭaṉ uvakaiyey tiṉāṉ” eṉṟār. puṇarcci yiṉpamē neñcil eññāṉṟum nilavap puṇarntaṉareṉṟaṟku, “uḷaṅkoḷap puṇarntu”eṉac ciṟappittār; itaṉāṟpayaṉ, nallaṟivu kāṭṭum naṉṉeṟiyaik kaṭaipōkac cellum kaṭaippiṭi avaṉpāl iṉmai yuṇarttuvatu.
yacōtaraṉukku yacōmatiyeṉṉum makaṉ piṟattal
78.	iḷaiyavaḷ eḻilnalam ēntu koṅkaiyiṉ
	viḷaipaya ṉicōtaraṉ viḻaintu cellunāḷ*
	kiḷaiyava ruvakaiyiṟ keḻuma īṉṟaṉaḷ
	vaḷaiyava ḷicōmati maintaṉ taṉṉaiyē.
urai:- icōtaraṉ - yacōtaraṉākiya iḷavaracaṉ, iḷai yavaḷ – iḷamai yuṭaiyaḷāṉa amiḻtamatiyiṉ, eḻil - aḻakaiyum, nalam - kuṇañceykaikaḷiṉ nalattaiyum, ēntu koṅkaiyiṉ viḷaipayaṉ - uyarnta mulaiyiṭattē peṟum iṉpattaiyum, viḻaintu - virumpi, cellum nāḷ - iṉitirukkuṅkālattil, kiḷaiyavar - cuṟṟattār, uvakaiyiṉ keḻuma - makiḻcciyāl āravārikka, vaḷaiyavaḷ - vaḷaiyaṇinta avvamirtamati, icōmati maintaṉ taṉṉai - yacō matiyeṉṉum makaṉai, īṉṟaṉaḷ - peṟṟāḷ, e-ṟu.
yacōtaraṉ amiḻtamatiyiṉ iḷamai, eḻil, ēntu koṅkai eṉṟa ivaṟṟiṉ nalattai viḻaintu iṉpuṟṟoḻukiyatum, av voḻukkattiṉ payaṉāka, yacōmatiyaip peṟṟatum itaṉkaṭ kūṟiṉār. kāmaviṉpattukkuc ciṟapputtaruvatu iḷamaiyātaliṉ, “iḷaiyavaḷ” eṉṟeṭuttu, amiḻtamatiyiṉ piṟa eḻil nalaṅkaḷaiyum koṅkaippayaṉaiyum vitantōtiṉār. yacōmatiyiṉ piṟappu acōkaṉatu vaḻiyeñcāmaikku ākkamātaliṉ, “kiḷaiyavar uvakaiyiṟ keḻuma vīṉṟaṉaḷ” eṉṟār. ivvāṟu maṉaikku viḷakkākiya amiḻtamati taṉakku viḷakkan tarum makaṉaip peṟṟā ḷeṉṟaṟku, “vaḷaiyavaḷ icōmatimaintaṉ taṉṉai yīṉṟaṉaḷ” eṉa avaḷmē lēṟṟiṉār. avaḷuṭaiya eḻilnalac ciṟappai, yacōtaraṉ mēlum, makaṟpēṟṟiṉai avaḷmēlum kūṟiyatu, piṉṉar avaḷ taṉ nalattaip poruntā voḻukkattil īṭupaṭuttik keṭuvatum, yacōmati ciṟappuṟṟu mēmpaṭuvatum kuṟikkoṇṭu pōlum. vaḷaiyavaḷ - cuṭṭu.
acōkaṉ taṉakku mutumai varavu kāṇṭal
79.	maṟṟornāḷ maṉṉavaṉ makiḻntu kaṇṇaṭi
	paṟṟuvā ṉaṭitoḻap* paṭiva nōkkuvāṉ
	oṟṟaivār kuḻaṉmayi rucci veṇmaiyai
	uṟṟuṟā vakaiyatai yuḷaintu kaṇṭaṉaṉ.
urai:- orunāḷ - ivvāṟu celluṅkālattu oru nāḷ, maṉṉavaṉ - vēntaṉākiya acōkaṉ, kaṇṇaṭi paṟṟuvāṉ - kaṇṇāṭi yēntikkāṭṭuvōṉ, aṭitoḻa aṭipaṇintu ataṉaik kāṭṭa, paṭivam makiḻntu nōkkuvāṉ - taṉ vaṭivattaik kāṇavirumpi atiṟ kāṇpavaṉ- vārkuḻal oṟṟaimayir - nīṇṭa kaṭaikuḻaṉ ṟamayiroṉṟu, ucci - nuṉiyil, veṇmaiyai uṟṟu - veḷḷai niṟattaip porunti, uṟāvakaiyatai - puṟattē naṉku tōṉṟāvakai maṟaintiruppatai, uḷaintu kaṇṭaṉaṉ - maṉam varuntip pārttāṉ e-ṟu.
paṇṭaiyaracarpāl aṭaippai tāṅkuvōr, aṭiyīṭēntu vōr, kaṇṇaṭiyēntuvōr eṉak kuṟṟēval ceyvār palar iruntamaiyiṉ īṇṭuk kaṇṇaṭiyēntuvōṉaik kuṟippittār. taṉṉai naṉku oppaṉai ceytukoḷḷak karutik kaṇṇāṭi nōkkuṅkāl; makiḻcci piṟattal iyalpātalpaṟṟi, “makiḻntu nōkkuvāṉ” eṉṟār. avaṉ makiḻcci muṟṟum varuttamāy māṟiṟ ṟeṉpatu, “uḷaintu kaṇṭaṉaṉ” eṉpataṉāl peṟṟām. oru mayir nuṉiyil naraittut taṉ naraipput tōṉṟāvakai maṟait tiruppiṉum ataṉaik kaṇṭu kaḷaintavaṉ, maṉattē varuttamuṟṟāṉeṉa vaṟika. ataṟkuriya kāraṇattai mēlvarum pāṭṭukkaḷāl uraik kiṉṟār.
acōkaṉ taṉmaṉattē palapaṭa niṉaittal
80.vaṇṭaḷir puraitiru mēṉi mātarār
	kaṇṭaka luṟavaru kaḻiya mūppitu
	uṇṭeṉi luḷaintika luruva villitaṉ
	vaṇṭuḷa kaṇaipayaṉ maṉitark keṉṟaṉaṉ
urai:- vaṇtaḷirpurai - vaḷaviya māntaḷirpōlum, tiru mēṉimātarār  - aḻakiyamēṉiyuṭaiyamakaḷir, kaṇṭu - pārttavuṭaṉ, akaluṟa - aruvaruttu nīṅkumpaṭiyāka, varu - varukiṉṟa, kaḻiyamūppu - āṇṭu mikutiyaiyuṭaiya mutumaip paruvamāṉatu, uṇṭueṉil - uḷatāyiṉ atu varaiyiṟṟāṉ, uḷaintu - maṉamtirintu, ikal - māṟupāṭṭiṉaiyuṭaiya, uruva villitaṉ - aḻakiya villiṉai yuṭaiya maṉmataṉatu, vaṇṭuuḷa kaṇai - vaṇṭumoykkum pūvākiya ampu, maṉitarkkup payaṉ - makkaṭkuk kāmavēṭṭaiyākiya payaṉai viḷaivikkum, eṉṟaṉaṉ - eṉṟu taṉakkuḷḷē collik koṇṭāṉ e-ṟu.
taḷireṉap potuppaṭakkūṟiṉamaiyiṉ, ciṟappuṭaiya māntaḷir koḷḷappaṭṭatu; “māviṉ avirtaḷirpōlum mēṉiyar”1 eṉṟu cāṉṟōrum kūṟutal kāṇka. kaṇṭār maṉam kavarum aḻaku tikaḻum mēṉiyait “tirumēṉi” yeṉṟār. mikka kātalarāyiṉum, mūp peytiyavaḻi akkātalāṟ peṟum kāmaviṉpamkuṉṟutaliṉ, avar kāmak kātalum nīṅkum eṉpār, “kaṇṭu akaluṟa” eṉṟum, yāṇṭu mikamika mūppumikutaliṉ, “kaḻiya mūppu” eṉṟum kūṟiṉār. eṉil-eṉṟāl. kāmavēṭkaiyuṟṟārkku maṉattuṉpam iyalpātaliṉ “uḷaintu” eṉṟār. kāmaṉ uruviliyāyiṉum, avaṉ vil uru vuṭaittāya, cāṉṟōrkku accam taruvatātaliṉ, “uruvavilli” eṉṟār. vaṇṭuḷa kaṇai - vaṇṭu taṅkiyirukkum pūṅkaṇai. malark kaṇai, kāma vēṭkaiyaik kiḷarac ceyvatu, payaṉātaliṉ, ataṉai, “vaṇṭuḷakaṇai payaṉ” eṉṟāṉ.
acōkaṉ iḷamaiyiṉ vaḷamaiyiṉmaiyait 
taṉṉuḷ niṉaittal
81.iḷamaiyi ṉiyalpitu vāku meṉṉiṉiv*
	vaḷamaiyi liḷamaiyai maṉattu vaippateṉ
	kiḷaimaiyu maṉaiyatē keḻumu nammuḷat
	taḷaimaiyai viṭuvatē takuva tāmiṉi.
urai:- iḷamaiyiṉ iyalpu ituvākum eṉṉiṉ - iḷamaip paruvattiṉ iyalpu ittaṉmaittākumāyiṉ, ivvaḷamaiyil iḷamaiyai - ivvaḷappamillāta iḷamaip paruvattai, maṉattu vaippatu eṉ-makkaḷ tam maṉattē poruḷāka matittukkoḷvatu eṟṟukku, kiḷaimaiyum aṉṉatē - ēṉaiyōr uṟavumuṟaimaiyum attaṉmaittēyākum, iṉi-iṉimēl, nam uḷam keḻumum taḷaimaiyai - nammaṉattē eḻum aṉpiṉai, viṭuvatu takuvatām - paṟṟaṟaviṭṭoḻippatē cālpākum e-ṟu.
iḷamaikuṉṟātu iṉpattiṟ cemmān tirukkiṉṟēmeṉat tarukkiya vuṇarvāl makiḻcciyuṟṟuk kaṇṇāṭi nōkkiya vēntaṟku mayiriṉ naraitōṉṟi avviḷamaiyiṉ kaḻiviṉaik kāṭṭakkaṇṭu, ataṉ nilaiyāmaiyiṉaiyum tērntu kūṟukiṉṟāṉātaliṉ, “iḷamaiyiṉiyalpituvākum eṉṉiṉ” eṉṟāṉ. iḷamaiyē vaḷamai yeṉṟum, “iḷamaiyiṟ ciṟantavaḷamaiyumillai”1 eṉṟum kūṟappaṭuvaṉa koṇṭu, ataṉaip pēṇutalum ulakiyalātaliṉ, ataṉai muṉintu, “ivvaḷamaiyil iḷamaiyai maṉattu vaippatu eṉ” eṉṟāṉ; eṉavē, iḷamaiyiṉ vaḷamaiyāvatu, nilai pēṟuṭaittāy iṉpa nukarccittāy iruttaleṉpatum, atukaḻinta vaḻic celvamum iṉpantārā teṉpatum peṟṟām “iḷamai kaḻinta piṉṟai vaḷamai, kāmam tarutalumiṉṟē”2 eṉṟu piṟarum kūṟutal kāṇka. maṉaivi makkaḷ aṭiyākap piṟakkum kiḷaiñar uṟavum, aracu, celvam mutaliya paṟṟukkōṭāka, “uṟiṉ naṭṭu aṟiṉ” oruvum iyalpiṟṟātalai yuṇarntu niṟṟaliṉ, “kiḷaimaiyum aṉaiyatē” eṉṟāṉ. aṉpiṉ payaṉ, ataṉaic ceyvōrum ceyyappaṭuvōrum piṇippuṇṭu niṟṟalātaliṉ, ataṉait “taḷaimai”yeṉṟum, atukāraṇamāka vēṇṭutal vēṇṭāmai tōṉṟit tuṉpam viḷaivittaliṉ, “viṭuvatu takuvatām” eṉṟum kūṟiṉāṉ. ēkāram - iraṇṭum tēṟṟam.
puṇṇiyamē ceyaṟpāṟṟeṉa acōkaṉ 
niṉaintu teḷital
82.muntucey nalviṉai muḷaippa vittalaic
	cintaicey poruḷoṭu celva meytiṉām
	muntaiyiṉ mummaṭi muyaliṟ* puṇṇiyam
	intira vulakamum eytalā meṉā†
urai:- muntu cey nalviṉai - muṉṉaip piṟaviyiṟ ceyta nalviṉaiyāṉatu, it talai muḷaippa - ippiṟaviyil taṉ payaṉait tōṟṟuvikka, cintaicey poruḷoṭu - uyarntōr matikkattakka nallaṟivuṭaṉ; celvam eytiṉām - araca pōkattaiyum aṭaintuḷḷōm, muntaiyiṉ muṉṉaiyiṉum, mummaṭi puṇṇiyam  muyaliṉ - mūṉṟu maṭaṅku mikutiyāka nalviṉaiyaic ceyyiṉ, intira vulakamum eytalām - tēvarulakattup peṟum pōkattaiyum peṟalām, eṉā - eṉṟu e-ṟu.
oruvaṉ ceyyum viṉai avaṉ uyiriṉkaṭkiṭantu, avaṉ pukkuḻippukum iyalpiṟṟeṉpavātaliṉ, “muntuceynalviṉai muḷaippa vittalai” eṉṟār. “muḷaippa” eṉṟa kuṟippu, nalviṉai yākiyavitai uyirākiyanilattu vitaikkappeṟutalai yuṇartti niṉṟatu; “vevviṉai ceyyumāntar uyireṉum nilattuvitti avviṉaiviḷaiyuḷuṇṇum avviṭattu”1 eṉṟu piṟarum kūṟutal kāṇka. oruvaṉ cintaiceyvaṉa, immaikkaṇ pukaḻum maṟumaikkaṇ puṇṇiyamum vīṭupēṟumātaliṉ, avaṟṟiṟ kētuvāya pukaḻum uṟutip poruḷaṟivum kūṟuvār, “cintai cey poruḷoṭu celvam eytiṉām” eṉṟār. celvattiṉum poruḷciṟantu niṟṟaliṉ, “poruḷoṭu ” eṉṟār. piṟanta piṟappāl, iṟanta piṟaviyiṟ ceyta viṉaiyiṉait terintu koṇṭāṉātaliṉ, “cintaicey poruḷoṭu celvameytiṉā” meṉṟum, “muntu ceynalviṉai” yeṉṟum kūṟiṉāṉeṉavuṇarka. mīṭṭum muṉ ceyta aḷavātal, irumaṭiyātal nalviṉai ceyyiṉ, maṟumaiyil maṇṇulakattē aracarpōkamum, avar ellāruḷḷum talaiyāya pōkamumē eytumeṉat teḷitaliṉ, “mummaṭi puṇṇiyam muyaliṉ”  eṉṟum, ataṉāṟpayaṉ viṇṇula kattuppeṟum intirapōkam eytumeṉṟu kaṇṭu, “intiravulakamum eytalām” eṉṟum taṉakkuḷ terinturaip pāṉāyiṉaṉ. ivvārāycci yuṭaiyavaṉ, nilaiyilpōkamākiya intiravulaka pōkattaiyum piṉṉart tuṟantu vīṭupēṟṟiṟkē muyal vāṉ eṉṟaṟika.
acōkaṉ tuṟavu
83.iṉaiyaṉa niṉaivuṟīi yacōta raṉeṉun
	taṉayaṉai nilamakaḷ talaiva ṉākeṉak
	kaṉamaṇi vaṉaimuṭi kavittuk kāvalaṉ
	puṉaivaḷai matimati pulampap pōyiṉāṉ.
urai:- kāvalaṉ - vēntaṉāṉa acōkaṉ, iṉaiyaṉa niṉai vuṟīi - ittaṉmaiyāṉavaṟṟai niṉaintu, yacōtaraṉ eṉum taṉayaṉai yacōtaraṉ eṉappaṭum taṉ makaṉai, nilamakaḷ talaivaṉ āka - nilattukkara caṉākuka, eṉa - eṉṟu colli, kaṉamaṇi vaṉaimuṭi kavittu - periya maṇikaḷaik koṇṭu ceyyappaṭṭa muṭiyaic cūṭṭi, puṉaivaḷai matimati pulampa - vaḷaiyaṇinta cantiramatiyākiya taṉtēvi taṉittu varunta, pōyiṉāṉ - araciyal toṭarpu tuṟantu ceṉṟāṉ e-ṟu.
“muntaiyiṉ mummaṭi muyaliṟ puṇṇiyam, intiravulakamum eytalām”  (82) eṉpataṉōṭu, immai maṟumai vīṭupēṟukaṭku ētuvākum iyalpuṭaiya viṉaittiṟamum, avaṟṟiṉ kaṭṭum, akkaṭṭiṉ nīṅkun tiṟamum, nīṅkāvaḻippaṭum tuṉpamum eṉṟa ivaṟṟaiyum palvēṟu nilaiyāmaikaḷaiyum piṟavaṟṟaiyum niṉaittamai tōṉṟa, “iṉaiyaṉa niṉaivuṟīi” eṉṟār. nāṭṭu vēntaṉai, nāṭākiya makaṭkuk kaṇavaṉeṉpavākaliṉ, vēntaṉā vāṉai, “nilamakaṭṭa laivaṉāka” eṉṟāṉ. colli yataṉōṭu amaiyātu, mantiriccuṟṟat tārōṭu kalantu tiru muṭiyum yacōtararkkuc cūṭṭiṉāṉ. eṉṟār. piṉpu acōkaṉ, araciyaṟ ṟoṭarpu nīṅki, maṉaiviyākiya cantira matiyaiyum tuṟakka veṇṇiyapōtu, avaḷ pirivāṟṟāmai kūṟi varuntavum avaṭku iṇaṅkātu tuṟavēpūṇṭu ceṉṟāṉ. eṉpār, “puṉai vaḷaimatimati pulampap pōyiṉāṉ” eṉṟār. vaḷaivuṉai mati matiyeṉpatu etukai nōkki “puṉaivaḷaimati eṉa vantatu” puṉaivaḷai” eṉa vaḷaiyai eṉa vaḷaiyai vitantōtiyatu, acōkaṉatu tuṟavu moḻi ceviyiṟ paṭṭamāttiraiyē avaḷ muṉkaiyiṉ nillātu kaḻaṉṟōṭi avaḷatu pirivāṟṟāmaiyai meyppaṭuttik kāṭṭiṟṟātaliṉ. iḵtu uṭampu naṉi curuṅkal. matimati - cantiramati. 
acōkaṉ kuṇatara muṉikaḷpāl aṟaṅkēṭṭut 
tavam ceyvāṉ vaṉattukkuc cellutal
84.	kuraikaḻa lacōkaṉ meykkuṇataraṟpaṇin
	taraicar kaḷaimpatiṟ ṟiruvar tammuṭaṉ
	uraiceya aruntavat* tuṟavu koṇṭupōy
	varaiyuṭai vaṉamatu maruvi ṉāṉarō.
urai:- kuraikaḻal acōkaṉ - olikkiṉṟa vīrakaṇṭai yaṇinta vēntaṉāṉa acōkaṉ, meykkuṇataraṉ paṇintu - meyyaṟi viṉaiyuṭaiya kuṇatara muṉikaḷai vaṇaṅki avar uraitta aṟaṅkaḷaik kēṭṭu, araicarkaḷ aimpatiṟṟiruvar tammuṭaṉ - tuṇaiyāy vanta aracar nūṟṟuvaruṭaṉ, urai ceya aruntavattu - collutaṟkariya tavakkōlam pūṇṭu, varaiyuṭai vaṉamatu - malaiyaiyuṭaiya kāṭoṉṟai, pōy maruviṉāṉ - pōyc cērntāṉ e-ṟu.
meyyaṟivupeṟṟut tavavoḻukkam ciṟantuniṟṟaliṉ, kuṇatara muṉikaḷai, “meykkuṇataraṉ” eṉṟār. ivarpāl acōkaṉ caiṉa tarumamum, caiṉa tīkkaiyum peṟṟāṉ eṉṟaṟkup “paṇintu” eṉṟār; tīkkaiyum aṟamum peṟumuṉpum piṉpum paṇitalviṉai nikaḻtaliṉ, “paṇintu ” eṉa uraikkappaṭṭaṉa; ittarumamum tīkkaiyum ācā rāṅkameṉṉum nūluṭkūṟap paṭukiṉṟaṉa; avai pañcaparamēṭṭikaḷiṉ patamupatēcittal, mayir paṟittal mutalāyiṉa. pēraracaṉātaliṉ, ciṟṟaracar nūṟṟuvar uṭaṉ ceṉṟāreṉavaṟika. tavatturuvu, tavavēṭam; “uṟṟanōy nōṉṟal uyirkkuṟu kaṇ ceyyāmai, aṟṟē tavattukkuru”1 eṉṟapaṭi, nōṉpaiyum uṟukaṇceyyāmaiyaiyum koḷḷalu moṉṟu. varaiyuṭaivaṉam, malaiyaic cārnta kāṭu; mūṅkil kaḷaiyuṭaiya kāṭumām.
yacōtaraṉ nāṭāṭci mēṟkoḷḷal
85.	erimaṇi yimaikkum pūṇā ṉicōtara ṉiruni lattāṭ*
	korumaṇit tilakam pōlu muñcayi ṉikku nātaṉ†
	arumaṇi muṭikoḷ ceṉṉi yaracaṭip paṭuttu yarnta
	kurumaṇik kuṭaiyiṉ nīḻaṟ kuvalayaṅ kāval koṇṭāṉ.
urai:- erimaṇi imaikkum pūṇāṉ - viḷaṅkukiṉṟa maṇi kaḷālākiya oḷivīcum aṇikalaṉkaḷaiyuṭaiyaṉākiya, icōtaraṉ - yacōtaraṉ, irunilattāṭku - periya nilamākiya maṭantaikku, oru maṇittilakam pōlum - oppaṟṟa aḻakiya tilakam pōlvatākiya, uñcayiṉikku nātaṉ - uccayiṉi nakarattukku aracaṉāy, arumaṇi muṭikoḷ ceṉṉi aracu - ariya maṇikaḷāl iyaṉṟa muṭiyaṇinta talaiyiṉai yuṭaiya aracarkaḷai, aṭippaṭuttu - taṉṉaṭi vaṇaṅkac ceytu, uyarnta - ōṅkiya, kurumaṇik kuṭaiyiṉ nīḻal - niṟamuṭaiya māṇikkaṅkaḷ vaittiḻaitta koṟṟak kuṭaiyiṉ nīḻalilē, kuvalayam - ulakam taṅka, kāval koṇṭāṉ - āṭcipuriyattalaip paṭṭāṉ e-ṟu.
acōkaṉ tuṟavupūṇa niṉaintavuṭaṉē yacōtaraṉai muṭi cūṭṭi aracaṉākkiviṭṭāṉātalāl, “uñcayiṉikkunātaṉ” eṉṟār. irunilattāḷ - nilamakaḷ, periyanakaraṅkaḷai nilamakaṭkut tilakamākak kūṟuvatu kavimarapu; “nilamakaḷ mukamō tilakamō kaṇṇō”1 eṉṟu kamparum kūṟutal kāṇka. nāṭukākkum vēntar ēṉaiyaracarait taṉ aṭippaṭutti uyartalē taṉ araciyaṟkuc ciṟappeṉṟum, valikku māṇpeṉṟum karutupavātaliṉ, ataṉaic ceytāṉ yacōtaraṉ eṉṟārāyiṟṟu. koṟṟakkuṭai veḷḷitāyiṉum māṇikkaṅkaḷum iḻaikkap peṟutalpaṟṟi, “kurumaṇikkuṭai” eṉṟār. kuvalayam - nilamākiya vaṭṭam.
pūṇāṇākiya icōtaraṉ nātaṉāyk kāvalpūṇṭāṉ eṉka. kuvalayaṅkāval - vikāram.
yacōtaraṉ celvac cerukkāl 
aṟattiṟam nekiḻttal 
86.	tiruttaku kumaraṉ celvac cerukkiṉāl nerukkap paṭṭē
	marutteṟi kaṭaliṟ poṅki maṟukiya maṉatta ṉāki
	urutteḻu ciṉattiṟ ceṉṟa vuḷḷameym moḻiyō ṭoṉṟi
	arutticey tarutta kāmat taṟattiṟa maṟattu ṟantāṉ.
urai:- tiruttaku kumaraṉ - tirumakaḷiṉ viruppam takkirukkiṉṟa aracakumaraṉāṉa yacōtaraṉ, celvaccerukkiṉāl - celvamikutiyāl uḷatākum mayakkattāl, nerukkuppaṭṭu - aṭarppuṇṭu, maruttu eṟi kaṭaliṉ - kāṟṟāl mōtappaṭṭa kaṭal poṅki alamaruvatupōla, poṅki maṟukiya maṉattaṉāki - avāmutaliyavaṟṟāl poṅkik kalaṅkiya maṉamuṭaiyaṉāy, uruttu eḻu ciṉattiṉ - mikku eḻukiṉṟa ciṉamutaliyavaṟṟiṉkaṇ, ceṉṟa - paṭarkiṉṟa, uḷḷam mey moḻiyōṭu oṉṟi - maṉam, uṭal, col eṉṟa mūṉṟum oruppaṭṭu niṟka, arutta kāmattu arutti ceytu - poruḷiṉpaṅkaḷilē vēṭkai mikak koṇṭu, aṟattiṟam aṟat tuṟantāṉ - aṟattiṉ vakaikaḷai muṟṟum kaiviṭṭoḻukuvāṉāyiṉāṉ e-ṟu.
puṇṇiyamuḷḷaḷavum oruvaṉaippaṟṟi niṉṟu, atu nīṅkiya vaḻi nīṅku miyalpuṭaiya tirumakaḷ, puṇṇiyamuṭaimaiyāl aracapōkan tuykkum yacōtaraṉpāl nīṅkātirukkiṉṟamai tōṉṟa “tiruttaku kumaraṉ” eṉṟum, tāṉ peṟṟa celvattiṟkut tāṉ mēṟpaṭṭa talaivaṉātalai yuṇarātu, ataṟku aṭimaiyāyt taṉ iyaṟkaiyaṟivai yiḻantāṉeṉṟaṟkuc “cerukkiṉāl nerukkuppaṭṭu” eṉṟum kūṟiṉār. “puṇṇiyamulantapiṉ poruḷilārkaḷaik, kaṇṇilar tuṟantiṭuṅ kaṇikaimārkaḷ pōl, eṇṇilaḷ ikaḻntiṭum”1 eṉṟum, “paṟṟiṉar pālaḷ”2 eṉṟum cāṉṟōr kūṟutal kāṇka. taṉṉaiyuṭaiyāṉ eḷiyaṉāyiṉ, avaṉai mayakkuvatu celvattiṉ ciṟappiyalpātaliṉ, “celvac cerukkiṉāl nerukkuppaṭṭā” ṉeṉṟumām; “maṉṉal ciṟitāy mayakkam peritākip puṉmaiyuṟukkum puraiyil arumporuḷ” eṉṟu vaḷaiyāpatiyum kūṟiṟṟu. “marutteṟikaṭal” eṉṟa uvamaiyāl, avā mutaliyavaṟṟāl yacōtaraṉ alaikkap paṭṭamai peṟutum. ciṉattai vitantōti yataṉāl, ataṟkētuvāya kāmamum, payaṉākiya mayakkamum koḷka. kāmavekuḷi mayakkaṅkaḷē ataṟku neṟiyāyiṉa maiyiṉ, anneṟikkaṇ avaṉuṭaiya niṉaivum ceyalum collum oruppaṭṭu nilaviṉa eṉpār, “ciṉattiṟceṉṟa uḷḷa meymmoḻiyōṭoṉṟi” eṉṟār. ivviyalpuṭaiyārkku vēṇṭappaṭuvaṉa kāmaviṉ pamum, ataṉai iṉitu nukarat tuṇai ceyyum poruḷumēyātaliṉ avaṟṟai, “arutti ceytu”, “aṟattiṟam aṟattuṟantāṉ” eṉṟār. arutti - ārvam.
poruḷiṉpaṅkaḷai arutti 
ceytoḻukum tiṟam kūṟal
87.	añcuta lilātu tevva raviyamē laṭarttuc ceṉṟu
	vañcaṉai palavu nāṭi vakuppaṉa vakuttu maṉṉaṉ
	puñciya poruḷu nāṭum puṇartiṟam puṇarntu neñcil
	tuñcuta lilāta kaṇṇaṉ tuṇivaṉa tuṇintu niṉṟāṉ.
urai:- tevvar aviya - taṉakku añcip paṇintoḻukāta pakaivēntar vali keṭumāṟu, añcutal ilātu - accam ciṟitu miṉṟi, mēlceṉṟu - paṭaiyuṭaṉ mikkuc ceṉṟu porutu, vañcaṉai palavum nāṭi - avar ceyyum vañcaṉai poruṭṭut tāṉum vañcaṉaikaḷ palavaṟṟaiyum nāṭic ceytu, puñciya poruḷum - avaruṭaiya mikkatākiya poruḷum. nāṭum - ataṟku varuvāyākiya nāṭum, puṇartiṟam - tāṉ kavarntu kōṭaṟkuriya cūḻccittiṟaṅkaḷai, neñcil puṇarntu - maṉattē ārāyntu koṇṭu, tuñcutal ilāta kaṇṇaṉ - nāḷum kaṇṇuṟakkam illāṉāy, tuṇivaṉa tuṇintu - ceyyattakuvaṉavaṟṟaic ceytu, niṉṟāṉ - attuṟaiyilē teḷi viṉṟi yoḻukuvāṉāyiṉāṉ e-ṟu.
pakai vēntarai veṉṟu oṭukki nāṭṭil nalakkuṟaivu nikaḻā vakaiyiṟ kāttal vēntarkku iyalpāyiṉum, acceyaṟkaṇ aṟamallatu nikaḻvatu eṇṇi yañcuvatu nalvēntar maṉappaṇpāka, ivaṉ, ataṟku añciṉāṉillai yeṉṟaṟku, “añcutalilātu” eṉṟum, appakaiyaracar vañcaṉai ceytārāyiṉum, aḵtu aṟamaṉ ṟeṉa voḻiyātu tāṉum avaṟṟaic ceytamai tōṉṟa, “vakuppaṉa vakuttu” eṉṟum, avarkkuriya poruḷum nāṭum kavarum tiṟamē niṉaintu, taṉ poruḷum nāṭum mikutaṟkēṟpaṉa vaṟṟai niṉaintila ṉeṉṟaṟku, “puṇartiṟam neñciṟ puṇarntu”  eṉṟum, niṉaivoṭu nillātu ceyaṟkaṇṇum ceytoḻukiṉā ṉeṉpār. “tuṇivaṉa tuṇintu niṉṟāṉ” eṉṟum kūṟiṉār. itaṉāl, “aṟattiṟam aṟattuṟan tāṉ” poruḷ ceyal kūṟappaṭṭatu.
88.	tōṭalār kōtai mātar tuyariyiṟ ṟoṭutta viṉpap
	pāṭalo ṭiyainta paṇṇiṉ icaiccuvai parukip palkāl
	ūṭalaṅ kiṉiya miṉṉiṉ olkiya makaḷi rāṭum
	nāṭakam nayantu kaṇṭum nāḷcila cellac ceṉṟāṉ.
urai:- tōṭu alar kōtaimātar - itaḻkaḷaiyuṭaiya pūkkaḷāl toṭukkappaṭṭa mālaiyaṇinta kūntalaiyuṭaiya pāṭal makaḷir, tuyariyiṉ toṭutta - yāḻnarampiṉāl toṭuttup pāṭum, iṉpap pāṭaloṭu iyainta paṇṇiṉ - iṉpattait tarum pāṭaloṭu iyaintu niṟkum paṇṇiṭattu eḻum, icaic cuvai paruki - icaiyākiya amutattaip paruki, palkāl - paṉmuṟaiyum, ūṭal aṅku iṉiya - ūṭiya viṭattum puṇarntāṟ pōlum iṉpattait tarukiṉṟa, miṉṉiṉ olkiya - miṉṉa laippōla acaintāṭum, makaḷir āṭalmakaḷir, āṭum nāṭakam nayantu kaṇṭum - āṭukiṉṟa nāṭakaṅ kaḷai virumpip pārttu makiḻntum, nāḷ cila cella - nāṭkaḷ cila kaḻiya, ceṉṟāṉ - oḻukivaralāṉāṉ e-ṟu.
itaṉāl iṉpattai arutticeytu oḻukum tiṟam kūṟukiṉṟār. tōṭu - pūvitaḻ. tuyari - yāḻnarampu; “tūmam kamaḻum kōtai toṭuttatuyarimulaiyāt, tēmeṉkītam pālāc curantu”1 eṉap piṟarum kūṟutal kāṇka. “paṇṇeṉṉām pāṭaṟ kiyaipiṉṟēl”2 eṉpavākaliṉ, “pāṭaloṭiyainta paṇṇiṉ” eṉṟār. kāmakkāta luṭaiya makaḷir ūṭutalum kāmattiṟ kiṉpamātaliṉ, “uṭalaṅkiṉiya makaḷir” eṉṟār; “ūṭiṉum puṇarnta tottiṉiyavaḷ”3 eṉṟu piṟarum kūṟuvar. paruki yeṉpuḻi - eṇṇummai vikārattāl tokkatu.
yacōtaraṉ amirmatiyoṭu kūṭi yiṉpuṟum ciṟappu
89.	maṟṟornāḷ maṉṉar tammai maṉaipuka viṭuttu mālaik
	koṟṟavē lavaṉṟaṉ kōyiṟ kuḷirmaṇik kūṭa moṉṟiṟ
	cuṟṟuvār tiraiyiṟ ṟūmaṅ kamaḻtuyiṟ cēkkai tuṉṉik
	kaṟṟaivār kavari vīcak kaḷiciṟan tiṉiti runtāṉ.
urai:- maṟṟu ōr nāḷ - vēṟorunāḷ, maṉṉartammai - aracavaikkaṇ vantirunta aracarkaḷai, maṉaipukaviṭuttu - tattam maṉaikkuc cellaviṭuttu, mālai koṟṟam - mālaiyum veṟṟiyu muṭaiya, vēlavaṉ - vēlēntiya vēntaṉākiya yacōtaraṉ, taṉ kōyil - taṉ antappurattēyuḷḷa, kuḷirmaṇik kūṭam oṉṟil - kuḷirnta oḷivīcum maṇikaḷāl iyaṉṟa oru kūṭattil amaikkap paṭṭirunta, cuṟṟuvār tiraiyiṉ - cuṟṟilum nīṇṭa tiraikaḷai yuṭaiya, tūmam kamaḻ - naṟumpukaiyiṉ maṇamkamaḻum, tuyil cēkkai tuṉṉi - paḷḷi yaṇai yaiyaṭaintu, kaṟṟaivār kavari vīca - kaṟṟaiyākavuḷḷa cāmaraiyai makaḷir vīca, kaḷiciṟantu - kāmakkaḷippu mikuntu, iṉitu iruntāṉ - amirtamatiyiṉ varavunōkki iṉi tiruntāṉ e-ṟu.
“maṉṉartammai maṉaipukaviṭuttu” eṉavē, iravuppōtiṉ varavu kuṟittavāṟu peṟṟām. pakaṟpōtil makaḷir toṭutta pāṭṭum, āṭiya nāṭakamum kaṇṭu kāmam ciṟantu antappurattaiyaṭai kiṉṟāṉātaliṉ, nērē paḷḷiyaṟaiyait tuṉṉiṉāṉ eṉṟār. “kuḷirmaṇikkūṭam” eṉṟār, kāmaveppattāl vetumpivarumāṟu tōṉṟa. kaḷi - kāmakkaḷippu. makaḷir kavari vīciyatu, kāmaveppattaik taṇittaṟku. iṉitiruntāṉ eṉṟār, amirtamatipāl tāṉ nukara virukkum kāmaviṉpamē niṉaintiruttaliṉ.
90.	cilampoṭu cilampit tēṉun tirumaṇi vaṇṭum pāṭak
	kalampala vaṇinta valkuṟ kalaiyoli kalavi yārppa
	nalaṅkalan tiṉiya* kāmar naṟumalart toṭaiya lēpōl
	alaṅkalaṅ kuḻalpiṉ tāḻa amiḻtamuṉ matiya ṇaintāṉ.
urai:- tēṉum - tēṉiṉamum, tirumaṇi vaṇṭum - aḻakiya nīla maṇipōlum vaṇṭiṉamum, pāṭa - pāṭṭicaikka, kalam pala - aṇikalam palavaṟṟōṭu, alkul aṇinta kalaiyoli - alku liṭattē yaṇinta mēkalaiyiṉ oliyāṉatu , cilampoṭu kalavi cilampiyārppa - cilampoliyoṭukalantu mikkolikka, nalam calantu - peṇmainalam mutirntu, iṉiya kāmar naṟumalart toṭaiyal pōl - iṉiya aḻakiya maṇaṅkamaḻum pūmālai varutalpōla, alaṅkal - pūmālai cūṭiya, am kuḻal piṉtāḻ - aḻakiya kūntalāṉatu mutukiṭattē tāḻntacaiya. amiḻtamuṉmati amutamati, aṇaintāḷ - yacōtaraṉirunta paḷḷiyai yaṭaintāḷ e-ṟu.
tēṉum vaṇṭum pāṭa, kalaiyoli, cilampoṭu kalavi, cilampiyārppa,kuḻal piṉ tāḻa. aṇaintāḷ eṉa iyaiyum, iṉik,kiṭantapaṭiyē, tēṉum vaṇṭum cilampippāṭa, kalaiyoli ēṉaikkalaṉkaḷuṭaṉ kalviyārppa, kuḻal piṉtāḻa aṇaintāḷ eṉiṉumām. tēṉ, vaṇṭiṉattuḷ oṉṟu, “vaṇṭukāḷ makiḻ tēṉiṉaṅkāḷ”1 eṉṟu piṟarum kūṟutal kāṇka. kalai, mēkalai. naṟumalart toṭaiyal aṇaivatupōla aṇaintāḷ eṉka; “maruvaḷarmālaiyor vallibṉālki yaṉanaṭai vāyn, turuvaḷar kāmaṉṟaṉ veṉṟik koṭipōṉ ṟoḷirkiṉṟatē”2 eṉṟu tiruk kōvaiyāruḷḷum varutal kāṇka.
91.	āṅkava ḷaṇainta pōḻti ṉaiṅkaṇaik kuricil tanta
	pūṅkaṇai māri veḷḷam porutuvan talaippap pulli
	nīṅkala roruva ruḷpuk kiruvaru moruva rākit
	tēṅkama ḻamaḷi tēmpac ceṟintaṉar tiḷaittu viḷḷār.
urai:- āṅku - avviṭattiṟku, avaḷ aṇainta pōḻtiṉ - avaḷ vantaṭaintapōtu, aiṅkaṇaik kuricil - aivakaip pūkkaḷālākiya ampukaḷaiyuṭaiya maṉmataṉ, tanta - viṭutta, pūṅkaṇaimāri - malarampu maḻaipōl vantu vīḻa, veḷḷam porutu vantu alaippa - kāmaveḷḷamāṉatu aṟivu mutaliya karaikaḷai alaittukkoṇṭu vantu avartam neñcai varutta, iruvarum pulli - iruvarum tammiltaḻuvi, nīṅkalar - nīṅkārāy, oruvar uḷ oruvar pukku - oruvar uṭaṟkuḷ oruvar pukuntu, oruvarāki - iruvarum oruvarēyāy, tēmkamaḻ amaḷi tēmpa - tēṉmaṇakkum paṭukkaiyacain tolikka, ceṟintaṉar - puṇarntu, tiḷaittu viḷḷār - oruvaroruvaraip piṇitta kai nekiḻātu aṇaimītē kiṭantaṉar e-ṟu.
malarampukaḷai maḻaipōlac corintataṉāl, ataṉ payaṉākiya kāmavēṭkai veḷḷamāyp perukutaliṉ, “pūṅkaṇai māriveḷḷam” eṉṟār. kāmam kaimmikkavaḻi aṟivum, niṟaiyum, piṟavum nīṅkutaliṉ, avaṟṟaik karaiyākki, “poruntuvantalaippa” eṉpārāyiṟṟu. oruvaruḷ oruvar pukkup puṇarum puṇarcciyai nākar puṇarcci yeṉpar. “nañcuṟṟa kāmam naṉinākariṟṟuytta vāṟum”1 eṉṟaviṭattu, “nākarōṭuvamai oruṭampātalum nīṅkalvaṉmaiyum paṟṟi” eṉa varum ataṉ urai kāṇka. viḷḷal - nīṅkutal.
92.	maṭaṅkaṉin tiṉiya nallāḷ vaṉamulaip pōka mellām
	aṭaṅkala ṉayarntu tēṉvā yamirtamum paruki yampoṟ
	paṭaṅkalan* takaṉṟa valkuṟ pāmpaṇai †puṇaiya tāka
	iṭaṅkaḻin toḻivi liṉpak kaṭaliṭai mūḻki ṉāṉē.
urai:- maṭam kaṉintu - iḷamai mikuntu, iṉiya - iṉiyaḷākiya, nallāḷ vaṉamulaip pōkamellām - nalla amutamatiyiṉ aḻakiya mulaiyiṭattē peṟum iṉpa muḻutum, aṭaṅkalaṉ ayarntu - aṭaṅkāta ārvattōṭu nukarntu, tēṉvāy amirtamum paruki - eyiṟṟiṭattūṟiya tēṉpōlum nīraipparuki, ampoṉ pāmpu paṭam kalantu - aḻakiya pāmpiṉ paṭattaiyottu, akaṉṟa alkul aṇai - akaṉṟa alkulākiya paṭukkaiyaiyē, puṇaiyatāka - teppamākak koṇṭu, iṭam kaḻintu - neñciṭam nirampi vaḻintum, oḻivil - nīṅkutalillāta, iṉpakkaṭaliṭai - kāmaviṉpak kaṭaṟkuḷḷē, mūḻkiṉāṉ - vīḻntu karaiyēṟa māṭṭātu ataṉuḷḷē mūḻki viṭṭāṉ e-ṟu.
maṭam - iḷamai; aḻakumām. muṉṉēkūṭi, yacōmati yeṉṉum makaṉaip peṟṟuḷāṉāyiṉum, yacōtaraṉ avaḷpāl vēṭkai yaṭaṅkāṉāyiṉāṉeṉṟaṟku, “aṭaṅkalaṉayarntu” eṉṟār. kaḻi kāmattaṉeṉpatu karuttu. eyiṟūṟiyanīr, “pāloṭu tēṉkalantaṟṟu”1 eṉac cāṉṟōr kūṟutaliṉ, “tēṉvāyamirtam” eṉṟār. parukutalai, atarapāṉam eṉpar. pāmpaṇai eṉpatu pirittukkūṭṭappaṭṭatu; alkulākiya pāmpaṇaiyeṉṟu kōṭalu moṉṟu. pāmpiṉ paṭamum puṇaiyum muṟaiyē uruvum toḻilum paṟṟi vantaṉa. iṭamkaḻital - neñcakam nirampit tuḷumputal, “iṭaṅkaḻikāma moṭaṭaṅkāṉāki”2 eṉṟu piṟarum kūṟupa.
puṇarcci vacattāl iruvarum kaṇṇuṟaṅkal
93.	iṉṉaric cilampun tēṉum eḻilvaḷai niraiyu mārppap
	poṉṉavir tārō ṭāram puṇarmulai porutu poṅka
	maṉṉaṉum maṭantai tāṉum mataṉakō pattiṉ* māṟāyt
	toṉṉalan tolaiya uṇṭār tuyilkoṇṭa viḻika ḷaṉṟē.
urai:- iṉ aric cilampum - iṉiya ōcai ceyyum paralkaḷai yuṭaiya kāṟcilampum, tēṉum - vaṇṭiṉamum, eḻil vaḷainiraiyum - aḻakiya varicaiyuṟṟa vaḷaiyalkaḷum, ārppa - olikka, poṉ avirtārōṭu - yacōtaraṉaṇinta poṉṉāli yaṉṟa mālaiyum, āram - amutamati yaṇinta muttumālaiyum, puṇarmulai porutu poṅka - puṇarcci nikaḻumiṭattu, mulaiyāl tākkuṇṭu mēleḻu, maṉṉaṉum maṭantaitāṉum - yacōtaraṉum amutamatiyum, mataṉa kōpattiṉ - kāmak kaḷimayakkiṉāl, tolnalam māṟāyt tolaiya - puṇarātamuṉpirunta mēṉinalam māṟupaṭṭu meliya, uṇṭār - kāmaviṉpattait tuyttu āyntārāka, viḻikaḷ tuyil koṇṭa - avaruṭaiya kaṇkaḷ uṟaṅkuvaṉavāyiṉa e-ṟu.
tār, mārpilaṇiyum mālai; iḵtu āṭavarkku urittu, tār eṉap pirittamaiyiṉ, āram amirtamati yaṇinta muttumālaik kāyiṟṟu. “maintar taṇṭār makaḷir peyyavum”3 eṉpuḻittār āṭavarkkurittākak kūṟappaṭutal kāṇka, kāmavēṭkaiyāl neñcu kalaṅkutalpaṟṟi, “māṟāy” eṉṟāreṉṟumām. “toṉṉalam tolaiyavuṇṭār” eṉṟār mīṭṭum avar ivviṉpam nukarā toḻivatu karuti. uṟakkattai avar viṉaiyākkātu, viḻimēl ēṟṟiyataṉāl, uṭal ayarntaṉarēyaṉṟi, uḷḷattāl kāmanukarccik kaṇ ayarvuṟṟilareṉṟu koḷka.
aṭṭapaṅkaṉ icaitta icaiyāl amirtamati 
aṟivu tirital
94.	āyiṭai yatti kūṭat tayaleḻun tamirta mūṟac
	cēyiṭaic ceṉṟōr kītañ cevipuka viṭutta lōṭum
	vēyiṭait tōḷi mella viḻittaṉaḷ viyantu nōkkāt
	tīyiṭai meḻuki ṉainta cintaiya† ḷuruki ṉāḷē.
urai:- āyiṭai - appōtu, attikūṭattu ayal eḻuntu - yāṉai kaṭṭumiṭattiṉ puṟattē eḻuntu, amirtam ūṟa - kēṭpōr uḷattē iṉpamuṇṭākumāṟu, cēyiṭaic ceṉṟa - neṭuntolaivu paṭarntu makkaḷ icaikkiṉṟa, or kītam - oru pāṭṭu, cevipuka viṭuttalōṭum - cevikkaṭceṉṟa taṉ icaiyiṉ pattaiyūṭṭavē, vēyiṭaittōḷi - mūṅkilpōlum tōḷai yuṭaiya amirtamati, mella viḻittaṉaḷ - taṉ kaṇkaḷai mellat tiṟantu, viyantu nōkkā - avvicaiyiṉ pattai viyappōṭu kēṭṭu maruṇṭu nāṟṟicaiyum pārttu, tīyiṭai meḻukiṉ - neruppiliṭṭa meḻukupōla, nainta cintaiyaḷ - melinta maṉattaiyuṭaiyaḷāy, urukiṉāḷ - urukalāṉāḷ e-ṟu.
āyiṭai - cuṭṭu nīṇṭu yakaram peṟṟatu. yāṉaikkūṭattiṉ kaṇ irunta aṭṭapaṅkaṉ eṭutticaitta pāṭṭu eṅkum parantu, kēṭpōr cevivaḻic ceṉṟu iṉpattai yuṇṭupaṇṇiṟṟu eṉpatām. ceṉṟa eṉṉum peyareccat takaram vikārattāl tokkatu. iṉṉicai ceṉṟu amirtamatiyiṉ ceviyiṭaiyicaittalum avaḷ tuyiluṇarn tāḷākaliṉ, “mella viḻittaṉaḷ” eṉṟār. viḻittāṭku avvicaiyiṉ iṉpam neñciṉai yurukki melivitta teṉṟaṟku, “tīyiṭai meḻukiṉnainta cintaiyaḷurukiṉāḷē” eṉṟār.
amirtamati matimayaṅkutal
95.	paṇṇiṉuk koḻuku neñciṟ pāvaiyip paṇkoḷ cevvāy
	aṇṇaluk kamirta māya varivaiyark kuriya pōkam
	viṇṇiṉuk kuḷateṉ ṟeṇṇi veytuyirt tuytal cellāḷ
	maṇṇiṉuk karacaṉ tēvi matimayak kuṟṟi runtāḷ.
urai:- paṇṇiṉukku - pāṭṭiṉ pattukku, oḻukum neñciṉ pāvai - nīrāy uruki yōṭum neñciṉaiyuṭaiya pāvai pōṉṟa amutamati, ippaṇkoḷ cevvāy aṇṇalukku - ivvicaiya mutattaic coriyum civanta vāyaiyuṭaiya periya vaṉiṭattē, arivaiyarkku uriyamakaḷir peṟutaṟkurittāyuḷḷa, amirtamāya pōkam - pēriṉpattait taravalla kāmapōkam, viṇṇiṉukku uḷatu - viṇṇulakattē peṟum pōkamākum, eṉṟu eṇṇi - eṉṟu taṉakkuḷḷē niṉaittu, veytuyirttu - perumūcceṟintu, uytal cellāḷ - uyvakaiyaṟiya māṭṭāḷāy, maṇṇiṉukku aracaṉ tēvi - avantināṭṭuk karacaṉākiya yacōtaraṟku maṉaiviyākiya ivaḷ, matimayakku uṟṟu - aṟivu kalaṅki, iruntāḷ - kaiyaṟavu paṭṭiruntāḷ e-ṟu.
oḻukutal - nīrā yuruki vaḻil; “oḻukunīr āralpārkkam kuruku”1 eṉpuḻippōl. muṉṉaic ceyyuḷil “urukiṉāḷ” eṉṟārātaliṉ, īṇṭu “oḻukum neñciṉ” eṉa vāḷāmoḻintār. nāṇvaḻi yoḻukum pāvaipōla, paṇṇiṉ cuvaivaḻiyōṭum neñciṉaḷātalpaṟṟi, “pāvai” yeṉṟār. itaṉāl maṉavuṇar viṉmaiyum peṟa vaittārāyiṟṟu. ceviccuvai yamutamākiya icaiyiṉai vaḻaṅkum ciṟappu nōkki, “cevvāyaṇṇal” eṉa aṭṭapaṅkaṉaik karutukiṉṟāḷ. aṇṇalukku, viṇṇiṉukku eṉpuḻi ēḻāvataṉkaṇ nāṉkāvatu vantu mayaṅkiṟṟu. amirtamāya pōkam, arivaiyarkkuriya pōkam eṉa iyaiyum. uḷatu - uḷatākiya pōkam. viṇṇiṉuk kuḷateṉṟu eṇṇiya tuṇaiyē, peṟutaṟ karitupōlum eṉṟu uṭkoṇṭu varuntaluṟṟāḷ eṉpār, “veytuyirt tuytal cellāḷ” eṉṟār. maṇṇiṉuk karacaṉ tēviyāy iṉpakkuṟai pāṭiṉṟiyē iruntāḷāyiṉum, aṟivaṟai pōki aṭṭapaṅkaṉpāl pollāk kāmavēṭkai koṇṭu maṉam puḻuṅkum puṉmaikaṇṭu ikaḻvār, “maṇṇiṉuk karacaṉ tēvi” eṉṟu vitantōtiṉār.
peṇmaiyiṉ puṉmai kūṟal
96.	miṉṉiṉu nilaiyiṉ ṟuḷḷam viḻaivuṟiṉ viḻainta yāvum*
	tuṉṉiṭu miṉattiṉ tūymai cūḻcciyu moḻiya niṟkum†
	piṉṉuṟu paḻiyiṟ kañcā peṇṇuyirp perumai pēṇā
	teṉṉumim moḻikaṭ kantō vilakkiya māyi ṉāḷē.
urai:- peṇ - peṇkaṭku, uḷḷam - maṉamāṉatu, miṉṉiṉum nilaiyiṉṟu - miṉṉalaikkāṭṭilum nilaiyillātatu, viḻai vuṟiṉ - evaṟṟaiyēṉum virumpumāyiṉ, viḻainta - avvirumpiyavaṟṟaip paṟṟiya, yāvum tuṉṉiṭum - niṉaivu ceyalkaḷaiyē poruntiyirukkum, iṉattiṉ tūymai - tuṇaiyā yiṉār kūṟum tūymaiyum, cūḻcciyum - ārāycciyuraiyum, oḻiya - keṭa, niṟkum - tāṉ cellakkarutiya neṟiyilēyē ūṉṟi niṟkum, piṉṉuṟu paḻiyiṟku añcā - piṉṉē eytukiṉṟa paḻi pāvaṅkaṭkuc ciṟitum añcātu, uyirpperumai pēṇātu - peṇṇuyirākiya tāṉ piṟanta kuṭipperumaiyum karutātu, eṉṉum immoḻikaṭku - eṉṟu aṟintōr aṟinturaitta ivvilakkaṇaṅkaṭku, ilakkiya māyiṉāḷ - ciṟanta eṭuttukkāṭṭāyiṉāḷ, antō - aiyō, ivvamutamatiyiṉ iyalpu iruntavāṟu eṉṉē e-ṟu.
“peṇṇuyir..... pērāttiṇmaiyum uṭaiyavalla” (47) eṉa muṉpē kūṟiṉamaiyiṉ, īṇṭu vāḷā uvamamukattāl, “miṉṉiṉum nilaiyiṉṟuḷḷam” eṉṟum, oru poruṇmēṟ cellum viḻaiviṉai nīkkiniṟuttum matukaiyillai yeṉpataiyum muṉṉar, “peruntiṟa laṟivum uṭaiyavalla” (46) eṉṟataṉai viḷakki, “viḻaivuṟiṉ viḻaintayāvum tuṉṉiṭum” eṉṟum kūṟiṉār. viḻaivu tōṉṟiya viṭattu, avviḻaiviṉai niṟaivu ceytu kōṭaṟkētuvāya niṉaivu ceyalē avar uḷḷattiṟ porunti vēṟu evvakaic ceyaṟkaṇṇum cellātoḻitaliṉ, “viḻainta yāvum tuṉṉiṭum” eṉṟār; viḻainta veṉṉum peyareccam karuviyoṭu muṭintatu. tuṇaiyāy oḻukum kuṇavati yeṉpāḷatu tūymaiyum aṟivuraiyum keṭac ceyyum ivaḷatu ceyal mēlvarum nikaḻccikaḷāl uṇarappaṭum. añcāteṉpatu- īṟu keṭṭu niṉṟatu. uyirpperumai, īṇṭuk kuṭippiṟappiṉ mēṟṟu, ivvamutamatiyiṉ - eṉpatu mutalāyiṉa kuṟippeccam.
kuṇavati eṉṉum tōḻi uṟṟatu viṉavutal
97.	tuṉṉiya iravu nīṅkat tuṇaimulai tamiya ḷāki
	iṉṉicai yavaṉai neñcat tiruttiṉa ḷirunta ellait
	tuṉṉiṉaḷ tōḻi tuṉṉit tuṇaivaril tamiya rēpōṉ
	ṟeṉṉitu niṉainta tuḷḷat tiṟaivinī yaruḷu keṉṟāḷ.
urai:- tuṉṉiya iravu nīṅka - poruntiyirunta irāppōtu kaḻiya, tuṇaimulai tamiyaḷāki - iraṇṭākiya mulaikaḷaiyuṭaiya amutamati ōriṭattē taṉittiruntu, iṉṉicai yavaṉai - iṉiya icaiyaiyuṭaiyaṉākiya aṭṭapaṅkaṉai, neñcattu iruttiṉaḷ - maṉattē kāmakkātalaṉākak koṇṭu, irunta ellai - vēṟuceyal maṟantu iruntapōtu, tōḻi tuṉṉiṉaḷ - uyirttōḻiyākiya kuṇavati avaḷaiyaṭaintāḷ, tuṉṉi - (aṭaintavaḷ) amutamatiyai neruṅki, iṟaivi - araciyē, tuṇaivaril tamiyar pōṉṟu - maṉakkiṉiya kātalar uṭaṉillāmaiyāl taṉittuppulampum makaḷiraip pōla, itu - vēṟupaṭṭirukkum icceyal, eṉ - eṉṉai, nī uḷḷattu niṉaintatu - nī niṉmaṉattil karutiyataṉai, aruḷuka - aṭiyēṉukkut terivittaruḷka, eṉṟāḷ - eṉṟu vēṇṭiṉāḷ e-ṟu.
“tuṉṉiya iravu nīṅka” eṉa vitantōtiyatu, aḵtu avaṭku ōr ūḻiyāyt tōṉṟi neṭitu kaḻintamai yuṇaraniṉṟatu. yacōtaraṉōṭu kūṭiyiruppavum, neñcilē “iṉṉicaiyavaṉai” iruttikkoṇṭamaiyiṉ, avaṉaiyiṉṟi yirukkumatu avaṭkut taṉimaiyāyiṟ ṟeṉṟaṟkut “tamiyaḷāki” eṉṟār. yacōtaraṉaiyum ēṉaittōḻimārkaḷaiyum nīṅkit taṉittiruntamai tōṉṟa, “tamiyaḷāki” eṉṟāreṉṟalumām. tuṉṉi yeṉṟa mikaiyāl, kuṇavati pōtarakkaṇṭum avaḷai varavēṟkātu tāṉ vāṭiya mukattuṭaṉ cāmpiyiruntamaiyum, avaḷ tiṭukkiṭṭu niṉṟu nōkki mella arukaṇaintamaiyum, amutamatiyiṉ maṟukiya neñcamum maiyalnōkkamum meyppaṭṭut tōṉṟiṉamaiyum peṟṟām. iṟaivaṉākiya yacōtaraṉuṭaṉ piriviṉṟik
kūṭiyiruttaliṉ, “iṟaili” yeṉṟum, pirivāl meliyum makaḷirkkuri mēṉi vēṟupāṭukoṇṭaliṉ, “tuṇaivaril tamiyar pōṉṟu” eṉṟum, kāraṇam kāṇamāṭṭāmaiyiṉ, “eṉṉitu” veṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ.
amirtamati uṟṟatu uraittal
98.	tavaḷavāḷ nakaiyāy* taṇṭār maṉṉavaṉ takaimai yeṉṉuṅ
	kavaḷamā rakatteṉ ṉuḷḷak karuṅkaḷi matanal yāṉai
	pavaḷavāy maṇikkai koṇṭa paṇṇiyal tōṭṭi paṟṟit
	tuvaḷumā ṟoruvaṉ ellit tuṭakkiṉāṉ ṟuyara† eṉṟāḷ.
urai:- tavaḷavāḷ nakaiyāy - veḷḷiya oḷiporuntiya paṟkaḷaiyuṭaiyāy, taṇtār maṉṉavaṉ - kuḷirnta mālaiyaṇinta vēntaṉākiya yacōtaraṉuṭaiya, takaimai yeṉṉum - kātalaṉ peṉṉum, kavaḷam ār - kavaḷattaiyuṇkiṉṟa, akattu - iṉpa voḻukkattaiyuṭaiya, eṉuḷḷam - eṉ maṉamākiya, karuṅkaḷi matanalyāṉai - kariya matakkaḷippiṉaiyuṭaiya nallayāṉaiyai, oruvaṉ - evaṉō voruvaṉ, pavaḷavāy maṇikkai - pavaḷam pōlum taṉvāyākiya aḻakiya kaiyiṭattē, koṇṭa - vaittuḷḷa, paṇ iyal tōṭṭi - icaiyākiya aṅkucattāl, paṟṟi - kaippaṟṟi, tuvaḷumāṟu - meliyumāṟu, elli - nēṟṟiravu, tuyarattuṭakkiṉaṉ - varunta vaṇakkip piṇittuk koṇṭāṉ e-ṟu.
collāṭaliṉṟit tuyaruṟṟirunta amirtamati cila collat toṭaṅkiyatum kuṇavati mukamalarntu muṟuvalittuk kāṭṭiṉa maiyiṉ, “tavaḷavāṇakaiyāy” eṉṟāḷ. ippōtu amutamatiyiṉ uḷḷam yacōtaraṉiṟ pirintu nīṅkiṉamaiyiṉ, piriyāmai muṉṉirunta nilaimaiyiṉai, “taṇṭār maṉṉavaṉ takaimaiyeṉṉum kavaḷam,” eṉṟāḷ. kavaḷam -  yāṉaiyuṇṇum uṇavu. ārtal, uṇṭal; “ārntōr vāyiṟṟēṉum puḷikkum” (kuṟun. 354) eṉpuḻippōla. akam, ākupeyarāy iṉpavoḻukkattaik kuṟittu niṉṟatu. icaiyavaṉ pāl kaḷaviṉāl kāmaviṉpam tuykkak karutukiṉṟāḷātaliṉ, avaḷ uḷḷattaik “karuṅkaḷima tanalyāṉai” yeṉṟār. yāṉaiyiṉ karumaiyai uḷḷat tukkum koḷka, kaḷaviṉāl tīya kāmam nukarak karututaliṉ; “kaḷa veṉṉum kāraṟivāṇmai”1 eṉac cāṉṟōr kūṟutal kāṇka. icaiyavaṉ vāyai muṉpu, “paṇkoḷ cevvāy” (95) eṉṟāḷātaliṉ, īṇṭu, “”pavaḷavāy eṉṟāḷ. yāṉai nallicaikku vaṇaṅkutal paṟṟi, neñciṉai yāṉaiyoṭu uruvakam ceytār, uḷḷattai matayāṉai yeṉṟum, ataṉai icaiyāl oruvaṉ vaṇakkiṉaṉ eṉṟum kūṟiyataṉāl, yacōtaraṉāl ataṉai vaṇakka
māṭṭāmaiyum, “iṉṉicaiyavaṉ” vaḻiyē celvataiyum kuṟittāḷāyiṟṟu. icai yicaittavaṉ aṭṭapaṅkaṉ eṉpatu aṟiyāmaiyāl, “oruvaṉ” eṉṟāḷ. “tuvaḷumāṟu” eṉṟum, “tuyara” veṉṟum kūṟiyatu, avaṉatu iṉṟiyamaiyāmaiyum taṉatu āṟṟāmaiyum uṇarttiniṉṟatu.
kaṇṭatu kaṉaveṉak kuṇavati kaḻaṟal
99.	aṅkava ḷakattuc ceykai yaṟintaṉa ḷalla ḷēpōl*
	koṅkaviḻ kuḻali maṟṟak kuṇavati yuṟaḻntu† kūṟum
	naṅkainiṉ perumai naṉṟē naṉaveṉak kaṉaviṟ kaṇṭa
	paṅkama tuḷḷi yuḷḷam parivukoṇ ṭiṉaiya‡ leṉṟāḷ.
urai:- aṅku - avviṭattu, avaḷ akattucceykai - amutamati taṉmaṉattē karutiyirukkum ceykaiyai, aṟintaṉaḷ - terintu koṇṭāḷāyiṉum, allaḷpōla - teriyātavaḷ pōla, koṅku aviḻ kuḻali akkuṇavati - tēṉporuntiya pūccūṭiya kūntalaiyuṭai yaḷākiya akkuṇavati yeṉṉum tōḻi, uṟaḻntu kūṟum - avaḷōṭu māṟupaṭṭukkūṟuvāḷāyiṉaḷ, naṅkai - naṅ kaiyē, niṉ perumai - niṉak kuḷḷa perumaiyō, naṉṟu - peritākum, kaṉaviṉāl - kaṉaviṉkaṇ, naṉaveṉak kaṇṭapaṅkamatu - naṉavupōlat tōṉṟak kaṇṭa iḻitakka nikaḻcciyai, uḷḷi - niṉaintu, uḷḷam parivukoṇṭu - maṉattil varuttamuṟṟu, iṉaiyal - varuntātē koḷ; eṉṟu uraittāḷ e-ṟu.
niṉaivuvaṭivākavē niṉṟoḻiyātu ceykaikkaṇṇum oṉṟi niṟṟaliṉ, eṇṇameṉṉātu, “avaḷakattuc ceykai” yeṉṟār. amutamatiyiṉ karuttaṟintum ataṉ tīmaikaruti vilakkum karuttiṉaḷātaliṉ, “aṟintaṉaḷallaḷēpōl uṟaḻntu kūṟum” eṉṟār. “uyarmoḻik kuriya vuṟaḻuṅkiḷavi”1 eṉa āciriyar tōḻikkum uritteṉṟaliṉ, tōḻi īṇṭu tēviyai uṟaḻntu kūṟukiṉṟāḷ. naṉṟē eṉpuḻi ēkāram etirmaṟaiyākakkoṇṭu, itu niṉperumaikkut tītu eṉṟu uraippiṉumām. paṅkam - iḻivu; īṇṭu iḻitakka kaṉaviṉmēṟṟu, iṉaivatu pētaiyār ceyal eṉpāḷ, “iṉaiyal” eṉṟāḷ. maṟṟu - acainilai.
amutamati coṟkēṭṭuk kuṇavati añcutal
100.	eṉmaṉat tivaru minnōy* ivaḷaṟin tilaḷko leṉṟē
	taṉmaṉat tiṉaiya vaṭkut tāṉurait tiṭuta lōṭum
	niṉmaṉat tilāta collai nīpuṉain taruḷiṟ† ṟeṉkol
	ciṉmalark kuḻali yeṉṟē ceviputait tiṉitu coṉṉāḷ.
urai:- eṉ maṉattu - eṉ neñcil, ivarum - mēṉ mēṉmikum, innōy - ikkāmanōyai, ivaḷ aṟintilaḷ - ivaḷ iṉṉum aṟiyavillai, eṉṟu - eṉṟu amutamati niṉaittu, taṉmaṉattiṉai - taṉ maṉattirunta eṇṇaṅkaḷai, avaṭku - akkuṇavatikku, tāṉ - tāṉē vāyviṭṭu, uraittiṭutalōṭum - uraittalum, cilmalarkkuḻali - cilavākiya pūkkaḷai yaṇinta kūntalaiyuṭaiyāy, niṉmaṉattu ilāta - niṉneñcil illāta iccoṟkaḷai, nīpuṉaintu - nīyē paṭaittuk koṇṭu, aruḷiṟṟu - uraittatu, eṉkol - eṉṉai, eṉṟu – eṉṟu colli, cevi putaittu - taṉ irukātukaḷaiyum kaikaḷāl pottikkoṇṭu, iṉitu coṉṉāḷ - aṟavuraiyaik kuṇavati coṉṉāḷ e-ṟu.
ivartal - mēlum mikuntēkutal. “ivartanteṉ mēṉi mēlūrum pacappu” 1 eṉpuḻippōla. kolliraṇṭaṉuḷ muṉṉatu aiyam, piṉṉa tacainilai. kulamakaḷirk kovvāta collai amutamati kūṟutaliṉ, “niṉmaṉattu ilāta col” eṉṟum, atu meyyaṉ ṟeṉṟaṟku “nī puṉaintaruḷiṟṟu” eṉṟum, atu nakaiyēyum vēṇṭaṟpāṟ ṟaṉṟākaliṉ, “eṉkol” eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ. “puṉainturai yaṉṟu; meyyē” eṉa amutamati vaṟpuṟuttiṉamaiyiṉ, kuṇavati poṟātu añciṉamai tōṉṟac “ceviputaittu” eṉṟum, aṟavurai pakarntamai viḷaṅka, “iṉitu coṉṉāḷ” eṉṟum kūṟiṉār. aṟavurai iṉpam payappatātaliṉ, “iṉi” teṉṟār; aṅkaṇat tuḷukka vamiḻtupōl payaṉpaṭā toḻin tamaiyiṉ, iṉiteṉṟē yoḻintār.
amutamati taṉatu āṟṟāmai kūṟal
101.	māḷavap pañca vappaṇ makiḻntava ṉamuta vāyiṟ
	kēḷala māyi ṉāmuṅ kēḷala mātu māvi
	nāḷava māki yiṉṉē naṭantiṭu naṭuvoṉ ṟillai
	vāḷaḷa vuṇkaṇ* mātē maṟutturai moḻiyi ṉeṉṟāḷ.†
urai:- vāḷ aḷavu uṇkaṇ mātē - oḷikalantamaiyuṇṭa kaṇkaḷaiyuṭaiya aṉpuḷḷa tōḻi, avaṉ amutavāyil - anta iṉṉicaiyōṉuṭaiya amutam poruntiya vāyāl, māḷavappañca vappaṇ - māḷava pañcamam eṉṟa paṇṇiṉ icaiyai, makiḻntu  kēḷalamāyiṉ - avaṉaik kūṭi makiḻntu kēḷātoḻivō māyiṉ, nāmum kēḷalamātum - nāmum tōḻamaiyiṉiṉṟum nīṅkiviṭu vōm, maṟuttu uraimoḻiyiṉ - nī eṉviruppattai maṟuttumāṟu kūṟuvāyāyiṉ, nāḷ - uyirōṭu kūṭivāḻum vāḻnāḷum, avamāki - payaṉaṟṟatāki, iṉṉē āvinaṭantiṭum - ippoḻutē uyir nīṅkiviṭum, naṭu oṉṟu illai - itaṟkiṭaiyē nammai vāḻvippatu vēṟoṉṟum kiṭaiyātu e-ṟu.
maṟutturai moḻiyiṉ, nāḷ avamākiya āvi iṉṉē naṭantiṭum eṉa iyait turaittuk koḷka. māḷavapañcamam eṉpatu oruvakaip paṇ; māḷava kauḷam, kētāra kauḷam eṉpa pōlvatu, iḵtu aṭṭapaṅkaṉ pāṭiyatu. kēḷāvaḻippaṭum payaṉ itu veṉ pāḷ, “nāmum kēḷalamātum” eṉṟum, maṟuttu moḻiyiṉ varum payaṉai, “aviyiṉṉē naṭantiṭum” eṉṟum kūṟukiṉṟāḷ. oṉṟu - nāḷ eṉpuḻic ciṟappummai vikārattāl tokkaṉa. vāḷ - oḷi, aḷavu - aḷavutal, kalattal. ivvāṟu aṟantiṟampiya neṟiyoḻukupavaḷ kēṇmaiyum uyirvāḻvum peṟutaliṉum peṟātoḻivatu peṇṇulakiṟkuc ciṟappāmeṉṟu karuti maṟuppiṉum maṟuppaḷeṉṟa eṇṇattāl, varum pāṭṭil, kuṇavatiyuṭaṉ mikanayamāka iv vamutamati pēcutalaik kāṇmiṉ.
avvicaiyavaṉ yāvaṉeṉṟaṟiyak kuṇavati tuṇital
102.	eṉṉuyirk karaṇam niṉṉō ṭiṉṉicai puṇartta* kāḷai
	taṉṉiṉmaṟ ṟoruva rillait takkatu tuṇika tāḻkkiṉ†
	eṉṉuyirk kēta meyti ṉitupaḻi peruku meṉṟāḷ‡
	tuṉṉumvā yavaḷō ṭeṇṇāt § tōḻiyu meṇṇi ṉāḷē.
urai:- eṉ uyirkku araṇam - eṉ uyirkkup pātukāp pāvār, niṉṉōṭu - uṉṉōṭu, iṉṉicai puṇartta kāḷaitaṉṉiṉ - iṉiya icaiyaippāṭiya akkāḷaiyēyaṉṟi, maṟṟu - vēṟē, oruvar illai - oruvarum illai, takkatu tuṇika - ceyyattakuvatu yātō ataṉaic ceyka, tāḻkkiṉ - kālam nīṭṭippiṉ, eṉ uyirkku ētam - eṉ uyirkku iṟutiyuṇṭākum, eytiṉ - avvā ṟaḵtu uṇṭāyiṉ, itu - ittāḻkkaiyiṉāl, paḻi perukum - peṇpaḻi tōṉṟi niṉvaḻivaḻip perukiniṟkum, eṉṟāḷ - eṉṟu amutamatiyurait tāḷāka, tōḻiyum - tōḻiyākiya kuṇavatiyum, avaḷōṭu - avvamutamatiyuṭaṉ, tuṉṉum vāy - avvicaiya vaṉait tēṭi yaṭaitaṟkuriya neṟikaḷai, eṇṇi - ārāyntaṟintu, eṇṇiṉāḷ - avaṉ irukkumiṭattait tēṭic cellat tuṇintāḷ e-ṟu.
“uyirkku araṇamāvār ataṟkuk kēṭu ceyyār” eṉpatu paṟṟi, “eṉṉuyirkkaraṇamāvār” eṉṟeṭuttu “niṉṉōṭu” eṉṟu uyarpiṉvaḻittākiya oṭukkoṭuttuc ciṟappittāḷ; oṭu - ōṭeṉa vikāram. tōḻivāyilāka icaiyavaṉaip peṟum iyaipu paṟṟi, avaṭku uyarpu tantāḷ eṉa vaṟika. palaraṟiya icaiyavaṉait tēṭik kāṇṭal amutamatiyiṉ karuttu niṟaivēṟaṟkākāmaiyiṉ, “takkatu tuṇika” eṉṟum, ataṉaiyum viraintu ceyalvēṇṭumeṉṟaṟku “tāḻkkiṉ ētam” eṉṟum, “paḻiperukum” eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ. iravup pōtil uṟaṅkuṅkālattē avviṉṉicaiyai amutamati kēṭṭāḷā taliṉ, icai vanta pakkam, icaiyavaṉuṟaiviṭam, ataṉ aṇimai cēymainilai, avaṉaik kāṇuntiṟam, avaṉai yuṭaṉpaṭu vittaṟku vēṇṭum coṟporuḷkaḷ eṉṟu iṉṉō raṉṉavaṟṟait tōḻi amutamatiyōṭu kalantē yārāyavēṇṭi yiruntamaiyiṉ, “tuṉṉum vāy avaḷō ṭeṇṇā” eṉṟum, eṇṇiyapiṉ eṇṇiyāṅkuc ceyvaṉa ceyyat toṭaṅkiṉā ḷeṉṟum kūṟiṉār. eṇṇuvataṉ payaṉ iyaṟṟalē yātaliṉ, “eṇṇiṉāḷ” eṉṟu upacarittār. eṇṇā - ceyyā veṉṉum vāypāṭṭu viṉai yeccam.
icai pāṭiyavaṉ aṭṭapaṅka ṉeṉṟu kuṇavati makiḻcciyuṭaṉ mīḷutal
103.	maḻukiru ḷiraviṉ vaiki māḷavap pañca vattēṉ
	oḻukiya miṭaṟṟōr kāḷai yuḷḷava ṉiyāva ṉeṉṟē
	kaḻuturu vavaṉai* nāṭik kaṇṭaṉaḷ kaṇṭu kāmat
	toḻukiya vuḷḷan taiyaṟ koḻiyumeṉ ṟuvantu mīṇṭāḷ.
urai:- iruḷ maḻuku iraviṉ vaiki - naḷḷiruḷ nekiḻum kaṭaiyāmattil taṉ irukkaiyilē iruntu, māḷavap pañcavattēṉ  - māḷavapañcamam eṉṟa paṇṇākiya tēṉai, oḻukiya miṭaṟṟu - corinta kaṇṭattaiyuṭaiya, kāḷaiyuḷḷavaṉ - kāḷaipparu vattāṉ, yāvaṉ eṉṟu - yāvaṉō  eṉṟu, nāṭi - tēṭic ceṉṟu, kuḻutu uru avaṉai - pēyiṉ vaṭivattaiyuṭaiya avaṉai, kaṇṭaṉaḷ - kuṇavati taṉkaṇkaḷāl pārttāḷ, kaṇṭu - avvāṟu kaṇṭa taṉāl, taiyaṟku - taiyalākiya amutamatikku, kāmattu oḻukiya uḷḷam - kaḻikāmattuṟaiyil perukiyōṭum maṉamāṉatu, oḻiyum - iṉi ataṉiṉṟum nīṅkum, eṉṟu uvantu - eṉṟu niṉaintu makiḻntu, mīṇṭāḷ - amutamatipālvantu cērntāḷ e-ṟu.
naṭuviyāmattē ceṟintirukkum tiṇiyiruḷ, kaṭaiyāmattē kaṭṭuviṭṭu nekiḻntu maḻukiyiruttaliṉ, ataṉai “maḻukiruḷiraviṉ” eṉṟār. avaḷ accamiṉṟic cēṟaṟkum atu naṉṟātaliṉ, maḻukiruḷai eṭuttōtiṉār. paṇṇiṉait tēṉāka vuruvakañ ceytamaiyiṉ, ataṟkēṟpap  pāṭutalai oḻukutaleṉṟār. ōr - acai. kāḷaiyuḷḷavaṉ yāvaṉ eṉpataṟku, kāḷai pōlvāṉāy uḷḷāṉ oruvaṉ, avaṉ yāvaṉ eṉṟu kūṟalu moṉṟu. kaḻutu - pēy. yāvaṉ eṉṟu nāṭik kaṇṭaṉaḷ eṉa iyaikka. kāṇappōnta kuṇavatikku amutamatiyiṉ kāmavoḻukkattiṉkaṇ viruppa miṉmaiyiṉ, aṭṭa paṅkaṉatu pēypōlum kāṭci aruvaruppai viḷaittatāyiṉum orupāl mikka makiḻcciyē viḷaittateṉpār, ataṟku ētuviṉ mēlvaittu, “kāmattoḻukiya vuḷḷam taiyaṟ koḻiyum eṉṟu uvantu” eṉṟum, “mīṇṭāḷ” eṉṟum kūṟiṉār. “mīṇṭāḷ” eṉṟataṉāl, iṉit taṉakku ittakaiya viṉai nikaḻātu; iḵtu ivvaḷavil muṭintoḻintatu eṉṟa karuttuṭaiyaḷāyk kuṇavati tirumpiṉamai peṟṟām; eṭutta viṉai muṟṟiṉallatu mīḷutal viṉai ceyvār toḻilaṉṟātaliṉ.
kuṇavati tāṉ kaṇṭataṉai amutamatikkuk kūṟal
104.	maṉṉaṉmā tēvi niṉṉai varuttuvāṉ vakutta* kītat
	taṉṉava ṉatti pāka ṉaṭṭamā paṅka ṉeṉpāṉ
	taṉṉaimey teriyak kaṇṭē taḷarntukaṇ putaittu mīṇṭēṉ
	eṉṉai nī muṉivai yeṉṉā vicaikkila† ṉavaṟkī teṉṟāḷ.
urai:- maṉṉaṉmātēvi - araca mātēvi; niṉṉai varuttuvāṉ - niṉ neñcu varuntumpaṭiyāka, vakutta kītattu aṉṉavaṉ - pāṭiya icaiyiṉai yuṭaiyavaṉ, attipākaṉ - ōr yāṉaippākaṉāvāṉ, aṭṭa māpaṅkaṉ eṉpāṉ - aṭṭapaṅkaṉ eṉṟu peyar kūṟappaṭuvāṉ, taṉṉai - avaṉai, meyteriyak kaṇṭu - uṭal muḻutum naṉku kaṇṭu, taḷarntu - maṉam cōrntu, kaṇputaittu mīṇṭēṉ - kaṇkaḷai mūṭik koṇṭu tirumpiṉēṉ, eṉṉai nī muṉivai eṉṉā - ceṉṟa karumattai avaṟku uraippiṉ eṉṉai nī vekuḷvāy eṉṟu añci, ītu avaṟku icaikkilaṉ - itaṉai avaṉukku yāṉ collavillai, eṉṟāḷ - eṉṟu kuṇavati kūṟiṉāḷ e-ṟu.
maṉṉaṉpāl oṉṟi avaṟku urimaippaṭṭirunta maṉam nīṅki, ayalāṉ oruvaṉmēṟ ceṉṟamaiyiṉ, amutamatiyai, aracaṉtēvi yeṉṉātu, “naṅkai” (99) eṉṟum, “ciṉmalarkkuḻali” (100) eṉṟum kūṟippōntavaḷ, iṉi, avvāṟu celvatoḻintu aracaṟkē yurittāṉamaiyiṉ, “maṉṉaṉ mātēvi” eṉṟāḷ. aṭṭapaṅkaṉpāl taṉakkuṇṭāṉa aruvaruppaik kuṇavati, avaṉicaitta paṇmē lēṟṟi, “varuttuvāṉ vakutta kītam” eṉṟāḷ. palavēṟupaṇkaḷāka vakukkappaṭutaliṉ, “vakuttakītam” eṉṟār. atti- yāṉai - mā - acai. aṭṭapaṅkaṉ vaṭivu muḻutum viḷaṅkakkaṇṭamai tōṉṟa, “mey teriyakkaṇṭu” eṉṟum, avvaṭiviṉ pollāṅkiṉait “taḷarntu kaṇputaittu” eṉṟu kuṟittum, ataṉaik kāṇiṉ amutamati aṟavē avaṉai veṟuppa ḷeṉṉum tuṇivāl, “eṉṉai nī muṉivai yeṉṉā vicaikkila ṉavaṟkī” teṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ. taḷarcci amuta matiyiṉ poruṭṭu.
aṭṭapaṅkaṉ vaṭivaik kuṇavati kūṟal
105.	narampukaḷ vicitta meyyaṉ naṭaiyiṉiṟ kaḻuta ṇaintōṉ
	tiraṅkiya viralaṉ kaiyaṉ ciṟumukaṉ ciṉavaṉ cīṟiṟ*
	kuraṅkiṉai yaṉaiyaṉ† kūṉaṉ kuḻittupuk kaḻinta kaṇṇaṉ
	neruṅkalu niralu miṉṟi nimirntuḷa cilapal leṉṟāḷ.
urai:- narampukaḷ vicitta meyyaṉ - narampukaḷ puṟattē tōṉṟumāṟu eḻunta meyyiṉaiyuṭaiyavaṉ, naṭaiyiṉil kaḻutu aṇaintōṉ - naṭaiyāl pēyk kōṭpaṭṭāṉai yoppaṉ, tiraṅkiya viralaṉ kaiyaṉ - tōlcuruṅkit tēynta viralaiyum kaiyaiyumuṭaiyavaṉ, ciṟumukaṉ - ciṟutta mukattaiyuṭaiyavaṉ, ciṉavaṉ - muṉkōpi, cīṟiṉ kuraṅkiṉaiyaṉaiyaṉ - ciṉantavaḻi mukam kuraṅkiṉai yoppaṉ, kūṉaṉ - kūṉiya mutukai yuṭaiyaṉ, kuḻintu pukku - kuḻintu uṭceṉṟu, aḻintakaṇṇaṉ - itaḻ puṇṇuṟṟu aḻukiya kaṇkaḷaiyuṭaiyavaṉ, neruṅkalum niralum iṉṟi - nerukkamum  varicaiyumiṉṟi aṅkoṉṟum iṅkoṉṟu māka, cilapal nimirntu - cilapaṟkaḷē mika nīṇṭu, uḷa - avaṉ vāyil uḷḷaṉa, eṉṟāḷ - eṉṟu eṭutturaittāḷ e-ṟu.
naram peḻuntu vicittatu pōlat tōṉṟaliṉ, “vicitta meyyaṉ” eṉṟāḷ. puḻuti yaḷainta mēṉiyum maruṇṭanōkkamum taḷḷāṭunaṭaiyum uṭaimaiyiṉ, “naṭaiyiṉiṟ kaḻutaṇaintōṉ” eṉṟāḷ. tiraṅkiya eṉpataṉaik kaiyaṉ eṉpuḻiyum kūṭṭuka. talaikkum uṭaṟkum ovvātu ciṟuttiruttaliṉ, “ciṟumukaṉ” eṉṟum, acciṟumai viraivil vekuḷum iyalpiṉaikkāṭṭaliṉ “ciṉavaṉ” eṉṟum. ciṉantu cīṟuṅkāl mukam peṟum vaṭiviṉai “cīṟiṟ kuraṅkiṉai yaṉaiyaṉ” eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ. kuḻinteṉavē yamaiyumāyiṉum, “pukku” eṉṟatu, neṭi tāḻntu kiṭantamai tōṉṟa. itaḻpuṇṇūṟṟu mayirutirntu cīyum pīḷaiyum nīrum oḻukutal paṟṟi, “aḻinta kaṇṇaṉ eṉṟāḷ; “cilapal” eṉṟum “nimirntuḷa” eṉṟum kūṟiyataṉāl; irukaṭaivāyilum muṉṉum mēlum kīḻum ivviraṇṭu paṟkaḷuḷa veṉṟum, muṉṉuḷḷavai mika nīṇṭu puṟattē veḷippaṭṭut tōṉṟiṉa veṉṟum koḷka.
iṉṉa iyalpiṉaṉāṉa ivaṉai nī vēṇṭaṟka 
eṉak kuṇavati kūṟal
106. pūtikan tattiṉ meyyiṟ puṇkaḷuṅ kaṇkaḷ koḷḷā
     cātiyun takka taṉṟā lavaṉvayiṟ ṟaḷaru muḷḷam
     nītavirn tiṭuka neñciṉ niṟaiyiṉaic ciṟaicey keṉṟāḷ
     kōtavitaḻn tiṭṭa vuḷḷak kuṇavati kompa ṉāḷē.
urai:- pūti kantattiṉ - puḻuti nāṟṟamum, meyyiṟ  puṇkaḷum - uṭaliṭattēyuḷḷa puṇkaḷum, kaṇkaḷ koḷḷā - kaṇkaḷ tāmum pārttaṟku uṭaṉ paṭā, cātiyum takkataṉṟu - avaṉuṭaiya kuṭippiṟappum takutiyuṭai yataṉṟu, avaṉ vayiṉ taḷarum uḷḷam - avaṉ poruṭṭu varunti meliyum niṉmaṉa viruppattai, nī tavirntiṭuka - nī viṭṭoḻittu, neñciṉ - avaṉpāṟcellum niṉ neñcattai, niṟaiyiṉai - niṉakku iyalpāyuḷḷa niṟaiyaik koṇṭu, ciṟaiceyka - taṭuttu niṟuttik koḷka, eṉṟāḷ - eṉṟu parivōṭu kūṟiṉāḷ, kōtu aviḻntiṭṭa - kuṟṟam illāta, uḷḷak kuṇavati - maṉattai yuṭaiya kuṇavati yeṉṉum, kompaṉṉāḷ - pūṅkompu pōlum tōḻi e-ṟu.
pūti - puḻuti. kantam - nāṟṟam. kāṇṭaṟku nam neñcu oru kāl virumpiṉum, karuviyākiya kaṇkaḷ ataṟku uṭaṉpaṭā eṉap pārttaṟku mikka aruvaruppuṭaiya avaṉatu uṭal vaṭivai yuṇarttu vāḷ, “kaṇkaḷ koḷḷā”  eṉṟāḷ. meyyiṟ puṇ kūṟavē, av vaṭṭapaṅkaṉ kuṭṭa nōyāl varuntutal peṟṟām. cāti, īṇṭu  iṟpiṟappiṉ mēṟṟu. neñciṉ eṉpuḻi aiyurupu, cāriyai niṟkat tāṉnilaiyātāyiṟṟu. niṟaiyiṉai yeṉpuḻik koṇṭu eṉṟu orucol varuvikka. niṟaiyiṉai yeṉpuḻi aiyurupaip pirittu neñcōṭu kūṭṭi, neñciṉai niṟaiyiṉāl ciṟai ceyka eṉṟuraip piṉumām. iṉi, neñcu il niṟaiyiṉaic ciṟai ceyka eṉṟu koṇṭu, neñciṭattē nillā toḻukum niṉ niṟaiyiṉai niṟuttuc ciṟai ceykai eṉiṉumamaiyum. kōtu, kuṟṟam. kuṇavatiyaik “kompaṉāḷ” eṉa vicēṭittataṉāl, amutamatiyākiya pūṅkoṭikkuk koḻukompāy niṉṟu, akkoṭi paṭara virumpum iṭattiṟkuk kompu tuṇaiceytu niṟkumāṟu pōla, amutamati virumpum aṭṭa paṅkaṉai aṭaintu makiḻtaṟku ikkuṇavati tuṇaiyāvatu peṟṟām.
kuṇavati kūṟiyavaṟṟai amutamati 
mēṟkoḷḷātu maṟuttal.
107.	eṉṟalu mivaṟṟi ṉāleṉ ṉiṟaivaḷai yavaṉka ṇārvam
	ceṉṟatu ciṟantu muṉṉē tiruvoṭu tiṟalun tēcum
	oṉṟiya aḻakuṅ* kalvi yoḷiyamai kulattō ṭellām
	niṉṟucey payaṉu nallār neñcamum peṟuta laṉṟō.
urai:- eṉṟalum - eṉṟu kuṇavati kūṟiṉatum, iṟaivaḷai - iṟaiporuntiya vaḷaiyuṭaiyāy. ivaṟṟiṉāl eṉ - nī kūṟiya ikkuṟaipāṭukaḷāl varum kēṭu eṉṉai, avaṉkaṇ ārvam ciṟantu muṉṉē ceṉṟatu - avvaṭṭapaṅkaṉpāl eṉ neñcam kāmakkātal mikuntu muṉṉamē ceṉṟoṭuṅkiviṭṭatu. tiruvoṭutiṟalum - celvattōṭu kūṭiya valiyum, tēcum - pukaḻum, oṉṟiya aḻakum - uṭalōṭu oṉṟupaṭṭut tōṉṟum vaṉappum, kalvi - kalviyaṟivum, oḷiyamai kulattōṭu - pukaḻ niṟainta kuṭippiṟappōṭu, ellām - kūṟappaṭāta piṟavumellām, niṉṟu - eñcāmal niṉṟu, ceypayaṉum - oruvarkkuviḷaikkak kūṭiya payaṉtāṉum, nallār neñcamum - aḻakiya makaḷiriṉ maṉamum ataṉ vāyilāka eytum avaratu pōkamum, peṟutal - peṟuvatām , eṉṟu colli mēlum kūṟukiṉṟāḷ e-ṟu.
kuṇavati kūṟiya kuṟṟaṅkaḷaik kēṭṭa amutamati, avaḷai maṟukkumāṟṟāl, kuṇameṉak karutappaṭuvaṉavaṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟaik kūṟaluṟṟu, tiruvum, tiṟalum, tēcum, aḻakum, kalviyum, oḷiyum, kulaṉum, piṟavumellām oṉṟi niṉṟu oruvarkkuc ceyyum payaṉ aḻakiya makaḷir neñcum avar tarum pōkamum peṟutalēyām; peṟāvaḻi avaiyum kuṟṟamām; kuṟṟameṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭaṉa tāmum appayaṉai eytuvikkumāyiṉ, kuṇamām eṉpatu kuṟippāl uraittāḷāyiṟṟu. ellām eṉṟatu, kūṟappaṭāta kuṇamellām eñcāmal taḻuvi niṉṟatu. payaṉum - eṉpuḻi ummai ciṟappu; neñcamum - eṉpuḻi ummai, eccap poruṭṭu; piṟa eṇṇupporuḷil vantaṉa. aṉṟō - acai.
108.	kāriyam muṭinta piṉṉuṅ kāraṇa muṭivu kāṇal
	kāriya maṉṟi teṉṟē karutiṭu kaṭavuṭ kāmaṉ
	yāruḻai yaruḷaic ceyyu mavaṉamak kaiya ṉāka*
	nēriḻai niṉaintu pōki nīṭalai muṭiyi teṉṟāḷ.
urai:- nēriḻai - nēriya iḻaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya tōḻi; kāriyam muṭinta piṉṉum - oruvaṉ eṭutta kāriyam muṭinta piṉṉarum, kāraṇa muṭivu kāṇal - akkāriyam toṭaṅku muṉ kāṇavēṇṭiya kāraṇamum payaṉum ārāytal, kāriya maṉṟu eṉṟu - ceyyattaku vataṉṟu eṉṟum, itu eṉṟum - ituvum atupōlvatē yeṉṟum, karutiṭu - uṇarvāyāka, kaṭavuḷ kāmaṉ - kaṭavuḷākiya kāmatēvaṉ, yāruḻai aruḷaic ceyyum - evaṉpāl taṉ aruḷaic ceykiṉṟāṉō, avaṉ namakku aiyaṉ - avaṉē namakkuk kāmak kaḻivaṉāvāṉ, āka - ātalāl, niṉaintu pōki - itaṉai niṉaittuk koṇṭu ceṉṟu, nīṭalai - nīṭṭiyātu, itu muṭi - itaṉai muṭittuk koṇṭu varuka, eṉṟāḷ - eṉṟu amutamati coṉṉāḷ e- ṟu.
oruvaṉ oru kāriyattaic ceyya vēṇṭiṉ, toṭakkattē akkāriyattukkuriya kāraṇaṅkaḷaiyum avaṟṟāl atu muṭivataiyum, ataṉālām payaṉaiyum cīrtūkkik kāṇṭal vēṇṭumeṉṟum, muṭintapiṉ kāṇṭalāl oru payaṉum viḷaiyāteṉṟum poruḷ nūl kūṟutaliṉ, “kāriya muṭinta piṉṉum kāraṇa muṭivu kāṇal, kāriya maṉṟu”  eṉṟāḷ. eṉṟu eṉpataṉai aṉṟeṉpataṉōṭum kūṭṭuka. itu veṉpuḻi eccavummaiyai virittu aṉṉatē eṉa oru col varuvittu muṭikka. iṉi, tāṉ mēṟkoṇṭirukkum ceyaṟku ōr amaiti kūṟuvāḷāy, makaḷir āṭavarpāl kāmaviṉpan tuyttalē poruḷākavuṭaiya reṉṟum, akkāmaviṉpattai niyamikkum kāmakkaṭavuḷ yāvarpāl ataṉaik kūṭṭukiṉṟāṉō, avarpāl ammakaḷir ataṉaip peṟaṟpālar eṉṟum colvāḷ, ‘kaṭavuṭ kāmaṉ yāruḻai aruḷaic ceyyum avaṉ namakku aiyaṉ’ eṉṟāḷ. muṉpu vēṟu niṉaivoṭu ceṉṟamaiyiṉ muṭittilai; ippōtu ikkaruttai niṉaintu ceṉṟu muṭikka veṉpāḷ, “niṉaintu pōki” eṉṟum , viraintu ceytal vēṇṭumeṉṟaṟku, “nīṭalai” yeṉṟum, muṭittukkoṇṭaṉṟi vaṟitu mīḷalākā teṉpāḷ, “muṭi” viraivu tōṉṟa “yitu” eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ.
kuṇavati maṉam naṭuṅki niṟṟal
109.	tēvinī kamalai yāvāy tiruvuḷat taruḷap paṭṭāṉ
	āvicel kiṉṟa vennō yarunavai ñamali yākum
	pūviṉvār kaṇaiya ṉeṉṉē puṇarttavā ṟitaṉai yeṉṉā
	nāviṉā luḷaintu kūṟi naṭuṅkupu voṭuṅki niṉṟāḷ.
urai:- tēvi - aracamātēvi, nī kamalaiyāvāy - nī tāmarai mēlirukkum tirumakaḷai yoppāy, tiruvuḷattu - niṉ aḻakiya maṉattāl, aruḷappaṭṭāṉ - virumpappaṭṭavaṉō, āvi celkiṉṟa - uyir pōtaṟkētuvākiya, vennōy arunavai - koṭiya kuṭṭa nōyāl uṟṟa tāṅkamuṭiyāta tuṉpattaiyuṭaiya, ñamali yākum - nāyiṉai yoppāṉ, pūviṉvār kaṇaiyaṉ - pūvākiya nīṇṭa ampiṉaiyuṭaiya kāmakkaṭavuḷ, itaṉaip puṇarttavāṟu - niṉṉai avaṉukku niyamitta vakaitāṉ. eṉṉē - eṉṉeṉpatu, eṉṟu naṭuṅkupu - eṉṟu maṉaṅkalaṅki, nāviṉāl uḷaintu kūṟi - nāvāl varuntic colli, oṭuṅki niṉṟāḷ - uṭal curuṅki niṉṟoḻintāḷ e-ṟu.
kamalai, kamalattai irukkaiyākavuṭaiyavaḷ; kamalam - tāmarai. puṇṇiya muṭaiyārai yaruḷuvatē tirumakaḷiṉ uḷappaṇpu eṉṟaṟkut “tiruvuḷam” eṉṟum, niṉ uḷattāl virumpap paṭṭavaṉ aṉṉaṉākātu perum pāvattiṉ payaṉāka veytum kuṭṭa nōyum koṭuntuṉpamum uṭaiyāṉeṉpāḷ, “aruḷap paṭṭāṉ āvi celkiṉṟa vennōy arunavai ñamaliyākum” eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ. aruvaruppu mikuti tōṉṟaṟku, “āvi celkiṉṟa vennōy” eṉṟāḷ. “pūviṉvār kaṇaiyaṉ itaṉaip puṇartta vāṟu eṉṉē” eṉṟatu, tirumakaḷai oru nāyiṭattē kāmaviṉpan tuykkumāṟu niyamikkum kāmakkaṭavuḷai ikaḻnta vāṟām. ivvāṟu kāmak kaṭavuḷai ikaḻntum, tēviyiṉ ceykaiyai veṟuttum, tāṉ avaḷ ceykaikkut tuṇaiyāka nērntataṟku varuntiyum veṟupput tōṉṟum coṟkaḷai vaḻaṅkutaliṉ, “nāviṉāluḷaintu kūṟi” yeṉṟum, piṉviḷaivu niṉaintu añci uḷaṅkulaivatu tōṉṟa, “naṭuṅkupu voṭuṅki niṉṟāḷ” eṉṟum kūṟiṉār.
kuṇavati aṭṭapaṅkaṉiṭam cellutal
110.	āṭavaraṉṟi mēlā raruvarut* taṇaṅka ṉāruṅ†
	kūṭalar tuṟantār‡ nōṉmaik kuṇampurin tuyartaṟ kākap
	pīṭuṭai yayaṉār tanta perumaka ḷivaḷeṉ ṟeṇṇit§
	tōṭalar kuḻali tōḻi tuṇintaṉaḷ peyarttuc ceṉṟāḷ.
urai:- aṇaṅkaṉārum - tirumakaḷai yotta makaḷir tāmum, āṭavaraṉṟi - tām virumpum āṇmakkaḷai yaṉṟi, mēlār - maṇantu kūṭutaṟkamaintavar mikka mēlōrāyiṉum, aruvaruttu - avarai veṟuttu, kūṭalar - kūṭuvatoḻittu, nōṉmaik kuṇam purintu - tavamum viratamum mēṟkoṇṭu, uyartaṟkāka - tēvarāyt tām viḻainta pōkattait tuyppatu kuṟittu, tuṟantaṉar - tuṟavu pūṇṭaṉar, pīṭuṭai ayaṉār - perumai poruntiya piramaṉāl. tanta - paṭaikkappaṭṭa, perumakaḷ ivaḷ - perumaiyiṉaiyuṭaiyavaḷ ivvamutamati, ātaliṉ ivaḷ tuṟattaliṉ iṟattalē tavaṟātu ceyvaḷ, eṉṟu eṇṇi - eṉṟu  taṉakkuḷḷē ārāyntu, tuṇintaṉaḷ - aṭṭapaṅkaṉ pāṟceṉṟu tēvi virumpiya kāriyattai muṭittuttara maṉam tuṇintu, peyarttu - maṟupaṭiyum, tōṭu alar kuḻali tōḻi - pūkkaḷai yaṇinta kūntalaiyuṭaiya amutamatikkut tōḻiyākiya kuṇavati, ceṉṟāḷ - aṭṭapaṅkaṉ irukku miṭattiṟkuc ceṉṟāḷ e-ṟu.
āṭavar, meyvaṉmaiyum iḷaimaic cevviyum uṭaiya āṇ makkaḷ, ummai - ciṟappu. ettuṇai mēlōrāyiṉum tam maṉam cellāvaḻi makaḷir aruvaruppareṉpatu ulakaṟinta ceyalātaliṉ, “aruvaruttu aṇaṅkaṉārum kūṭalar” eṉṟum, avar karuttaṟiyātu ammēlōraip puṇarttavaḻi ammakaḷir ceyyum ceykai kūṟuvāḷ, “tuṟantār nōṉmaikkuṇam purintuyartaṟkāka” eṉṟum, aḻakum tiruvum iḷamaiyum aṭaiya mikkiruttaliṉ, piramaṉ ciṟappāka muyaṉṟu paṭaitta perumaiyuṭaiyaḷ ivvamutamati yeṉṟaṟku, “pīṭuṭaiyayaṉār tanta perumakaḷ” eṉṟum, kaṇavaṉōṭu kūṭiyiruttaliṉ, tuṟavu pūṇpatai viṭuttu iṟattalaiyē ceyvaḷ eṉpāḷ, “perumakaḷ ivaḷ” eṉṟum kuṇavati eṇṇiṉāḷāyiṟṟu. “iṟappāḷ” eṉṟu kūṟiṟṟilaḷ, neñciṉuḷ ataṉait teḷiya vuṇarntu koṇṭa maiyiṉ. kuṇavatiyum amutamatiyiṉ karuttukku nērnta uṭam paṭṭamaiyiṉ, “tōṭalar kuḻali tōḻi” eṉṟum, tuṇiyātiruntavaḷ, ippōtu aṭṭapaṅkaṉ pāl celvatu takkatu eṉṟu tuṇintāḷeṉpār, “tuṇintaṉaḷ” eṉṟum, iṭaiyaṟaviṉṟi uṭaṉē cellutal tōṉṟa, “tuṇintaṉaḷ ceṉṟāḷ” eṉṟum, muṉṉorukāṟ ceṉṟiruttaliṉ, “peyarttu” eṉṟum kūṟiṉār. nōṉmai, tavam. kuṇam, viratam. tavamuṭaiyārkku viratam paṇ pātaliṉ, ataṉaik “kuṇam” eṉṟār. cīvakaṉai maṇakkak karutiyirunta kuṇamālaikku vēṟṟu varaivu pēcappaṭṭapōtu, “kāḷaikku entaiyum yāyum nērāviṭiṉ iṟattaloṉṟō, cintaṉai piṟi toṉṟākic ceytavam muyaṟaloṉṟō”1 eṉak kūṟutal kāṇka.
camaṇ camayattavar, makaḷirkku vīṭupēṟu illai eṉpa vākaliṉ,  uyartal eṉpataṟkut tēvarāy iṉpuṟutal eṉṟu urai kūṟappaṭṭatu. “viratacī  latta rākit tāṉameyt tavarkkuc ceytu, arukaṉa caraṇa mūḻki yāṉṟavarc ciṟappuc ceytu, karutu naṟ kaṇavaṟ pēṇuṅ kaṟpuṭai makaḷi rinta, uruvatti nīṅkik kaṟpat tuttama tēvarāvār” (739) eṉṟum, “mātavan tāṅki vaiyattaiyarāy vantu tōṉṟi, ētamoṉṟiṉṟi vīṭu meytuvar” (740) eṉṟum mērumantara purāṇam kūṟutal kāṇka. tēvar peṟum iṉpattai,
“āṭātu moḷitikaḻu māraṇaṅku tirumēṉi
 vāṭāta kaṇṇiyiṉar maḻuṅkāta pūntukilar
 ēṭārnta toṅkalarāy iṉpanīrp peruveḷḷam
 nīṭārak kuḷittāṭum nilaimaiyarē yavarellām” (tuṟavu 211)
eṉat tōlāmoḻit tēvar kūṟuvatu kāṇka.
kuṇavati aṭṭapaṅkaṉpāl tēviyiṉ 
viḻaivait terivittal
*111. muṭaippaṭu nāṟṟa mēṉi muḻutaḻuk kāṭai pōrttuk
	 kaṭaippaṭu tukaḷu maṇṇuṅ kañaliya kūṭat tāṅkōr
	 puṭaikkiṭan tuṟaṅku vāṉaip puḻuṅkiya maṉattō ṭaṇmi
	 viṭaipparun tāṉai vēntaṉ ṟēviyiṉ viḻaivu coṉṉāḷ.
urai:- muṭaippaṭu nāṟṟam mēṉi -  muṭaināṟṟam nāṟukiṉṟa vuṭampu, muḻutu - muḻutum, aḻukkāṭai pōrttu - aḻukkēṟiya āṭaiyāl pōrttukkoṇṭu, kaṭaip paṭutukaḷum - tūymaiyaṟṟatākak karutappaṭum kūḷamum, maṇṇum - maṇpoṭiyum, kañaliya kūṭattu - niṟainta yāṉaik kūṭattil, ōr puṭai - oru puṟattē, kiṭantu uṟaṅkuvāṉai - paṭuttuṟaṅkiya aṭṭapaṅkaṉai, puḻuṅkiya maṉattōṭu aṇmi - aruvarutta maṉattuṭaṉ arukē ceṉṟu, viṭaippu arun tāṉai vēntaṉ tēviyiṉ viḻaivu - vēṟupaṭuttaṟkariya tāṉai yiṉaiyuṭaiya vēntaṉāṉa yacōtaraṉukku maṉaiviyākiya amutamatiyiṉ viruppattai, coṉṉāḷ - cilavāya coṟkaḷāl kuṇavati mellac coṉṉāḷ e-ṟu.
mēṉi muḻutum puṇṇuṟṟut tīnāṟṟam nāṟak kiṭattaliṉ, “muṭaippaṭu nāṟṟa mēṉi muḻutum” eṉṟār. “meyyiṟ puṇkaḷum kaṇkaḷ koḷḷā” (106) eṉṟu kuṇavatiyum, “puṇ peṟṟa meyyaṉ” (113) eṉṟu avaṉēyum kūṟutal kāṇka. muṭaippaṭu nāṟṟam, muṭaiyuṇṭākum nāṟṟam. cīvaṭitaliṉ ī mutaliyaṉa moykkā vaṇṇam pōrttuk kiṭantāṉ eṉpār, “mēṉi muḻutum aḻukkāṭai pōrttu” eṉṟār. yāṉai tiṉṟu kaḻinta puṟṟukaḷum - vaittukaḷum ataṉ ciṟunīrāl naṉaintu ataṉ malam aḷaintu pularntu kiṭattaliṉ, “kaṭaippaṭutuka” ḷeṉṟār. kāṇṭaṟkut tuṇintu pōntāḷāyiṉum, avaṉatu mēṉiyum, āṭaip pōrvaiyum iṭattiṉ iḻivum kāṇakkāṇa avaṭku aruvaruppu neñci leḻuntamaiyiṉ, “puḻuṅkiya maṉattōṭu” aṇukiṉāḷ. pakaivarāl vēṟupaṭuttaṟkariya tāṉaiyātaliṉ, viṭaipparun tāṉaiyāyiṟṟu;  “viṭaipparun tāṉai vēntaṉ vēṇṭu veṟuppa nalki”1 eṉṟu tēvarum kūṟutal kāṇka. cila vāya coṟkaḷāl tam karuttai mellac collutal iḷaiya makaḷirkku iyalpātaliṉ, ataṉai eṭuttō tātu “viḻaivu coṉṉāḷ” eṉṟoḻintār; aṉṟi, eṭuttōtaṟku nāṇiyatu tōṉṟa uyttuṇara vaittāreṉiṉumām. vēṟupaṭut taṟkariya tāṉai koṇṭu nāṭṭukku vēṇṭum kāvalai naṉku ceyyum vēntaṉāyiṉum, avaṉ tēvikku akkāval payaṉpaṭā tāyiṟṟeṉpār, “viṭaipparuntāṉai vēntaṉ tēvi ” eṉṟār.
aṭṭapaṅkaṉ uṭaṉpaṭātu vekuḷutal
112.	kēṭṭalu mañcu neñcaṉ kēṭeṉak keytiṟ ṟeṉṟē
	vāṭṭamum naṭukku muṟṟē māṇpila moḻintāy maṉṉaṉ
	vīṭṭiṭuñ celkeṉ ṟēva vēypurai tōḷi tōḻi
	kāṭṭuva kāṭṭi yaṉṉāṉ karuttaiyuṅ kalaittiṭ ṭāḷē.
urai:- kēṭṭalum - kuṇavati colliyatu kēṭṭamāttiraiyē, añcum  neñcaṉ - añcukiṉṟa maṉamuṭaiyavaṉāṉa aṭṭapaṅkaṉ, eṉakkuk kēṭu eytiṟṟu eṉṟu - eṉakku nācakālam vantatu eṉṟu niṉaittu, vāṭṭamum naṭukkam uṟṟu - maṉavāṭṭamum naṭukkamu maṭaintu, māṇpu ila moḻintāy - tēviyiṉ perumaikkut takātavaṟṟaic colkiṉṟāy, maṉṉaṉ vīṭṭiṭum - itaṉai yaṟintāl vēntaṉ koṉṟuviṭuvāṉ, celka - nī ippōtē ivviṭat tiṉiṉṟum pōyviṭuka, eṉṟu ēva - eṉṟu ciṉantu kūṟa, vēypurai tōḷi tōḻi - mūṅkilpōlum tōḷkaḷaiyuṭaiya amutamatiyiṉ tōḻiyākiya kuṇavati, kāṭṭuvakāṭṭi - avaṉ aiyuṟātu ēṟkattakka cāṉṟukaḷai eṭuttukkūṟikkāṭṭi, aṉṉāṉ karuttaiyum kalait tiṭṭāḷ - avaṉuṭaiya maṉakkaruttaik kalaittut tēviyiṉ viruppattukku icaivittuk koṇṭāḷ e-ṟu.
iyalpākavē accamuṭaiyaṉātaliṉ, kuṇavati kūṟiyataṉāl atu mikuntu varuttuvatupaṟṟi “añcum neñcaṉ,” eṉṟār; “accamē kīḻkaḷatu ācāram”1 eṉpataṉāl, accam aṭṭapaṅkaṟku iyal pātalaṟika. aracaṉ kōyiliṟ paṇipurivōr aṭaṅkiya maṉamila rāyiṉ, kēṭeytal oru talaiyātaliṉ, “kēṭeṉak keytumeṉṟu vāṭṭamum naṭukkamum” koṇṭāṉ; “maṉṉar kōyil uṟaivār poṟi ceṟitta māṇpiṉarē”2 eṉṟu tirutakkatēvar kūṟutal kāṇka. tēvikkut tōḻiyāvāḷ avaḷ māṇpiṟku iḻukkut taruvataṉaic colvatu tī tātaliṉ, “māṇpila moḻintāy” eṉṟum, avaḷai accuṟuttip pōkkum karuttāl, “maṉṉaṉ vīṭṭiṭum celka” eṉṟum, eṉṟataṉōṭamai yātu, avaṉ collakkēṭṭum kuṇavati puṭaipeyarāmaiyiṉ, avaṉē avaḷaip pōkkak karutiṉāṉeṉpār “ēva” eṉṟum kūṟiṉār. vēypurai tōḷi tōḻiyeṉṟatu, tāṉ karutiyatu muṭittuk koṇṭallatu ēkāta iyalpiṉaḷātalai yuṇarttiṟṟu. ivvāṟu avaṉ maṉantiṟampāṉātalaik kaṇṭa kuṇavati avaṉ ēṟkattakka coṟkaḷaiyum, avaṟṟiṟkuc cāṉṟākak kāṭṭat takuvaṉavum vaḻaṅki, avaṉai matiyuṭampaṭuttāḷ eṉpār, “kāṭṭuva kāṭṭi yaṉṉāṉ karuttaiyum kalaittiṭṭāḷē” eṉṟār. pētaiyātaliṉ, piṟaṉil viḻaiyum it tīviṉaikku avaṉum icaintāṉeṉa vaṟika.
aṭṭapaṅkaṉ maṉaṅkalaintu makiḻnturaittal
113.	paṇpeṟṟa moḻiyāy yāṉum paṇpeṟṟa payaṉum peṟṟēṉ
	puṇpeṟṟa meyyaṉ pollāp puḻutiyiṟ ṟuḷaiyuṅ kaiyaṉ
	maṇpeṟṟa iṟaivaṉ tēvi maṉampeṟṟu makiḻvē ṉeṉṉiṉ
	eṇpeṟṟa tavamiyār peṟṟār yāṉpeṟṟa pēṟi teṉṟāṉ.
urai:- paṇ peṟṟa moḻiyāy - paṇṇicaipōlum coṟkaḷaiyuṭaiyavaḷē, paṇpeṟṟa payaṉ - paṇpāṭum aṟivu peṟṟataṉālaṭaiyum payaṉai, yāṉum - peṟaṟkākāta yāṉum, peṟṟēṉ - iṉṟu peṟṟēṉ, (evvāṟeṉil) puṇpeṟṟa meyyaṉ - puṇkaḷ niṟainta uṭampiṉaiyuṭaiyēṉ, pollāp puḻutiyil - nallatallāta puḻutiyilē, tuḷaiyum kaiyaṉ - puraṇṭukiṭakkum ciṟumaiyumuṭaiyēṉ, maṇpeṟṟa iṟaivaṉ tēvi - maṇṇula kattaiyāḷum aracaṉukku maṉaiviyiṉuṭaiya, maṉam peṟṟu makiḻ vēṉ - kātalaṉpu peṟṟu makiḻkiṉṟēṉātalāl, eṇpeṟṟa tavam - yāvarum eṇṇi virumpattakka tavappayaṉai, eṉṉiṉ - eṉṉaip pōla, yār peṟṟār - yāvar peṟṟār, itu yāṉ peṟṟa pēṟu - itu yāṉ aritākap peṟṟa pēṟākum, eṉṟāṉ - eṉṟu aṭṭapaṅkaṉ kuṇavatikkuk kūṟiṉāṉ e-ṟu.
ummai - ciṟappu. “puṇpeṟṟa meyyaṉ”, “puḻutiyiṟ ṟuyaiyuṅ kaiyaṉ” eṉat taṉ nilaimaiyai yuṇarntiruttaliṉ, “yāṉum peṟṟēṉ” eṉṟum, toṭakkattē paṇpilavāyt tōṉṟiya moḻikaḷ iṟutiyil iṉpap payaṉ tantamaiyiṉ, “paṇpeṟṟa moḻiyāy” eṉṟum kūṟiṉāṉ. taṉ nilaimaiyai nōkka aracamā tēviyiṉ iṉpappēṟu taṉakku vāyttataṟkut taṉ tavamēyaṉṟip piṟitoru kāraṇamum kāṇamāṭṭāmaiyiṉ, “eṉṉiṉ eṇ peṟṟa tavamiyār peṟṟār” eṉṟāṉ. aritiṟ peṟṟa pēṟu eṉa viyanturaittaliṉ, “yāṉ peṟṟa pēṟitu” eṉa vitantōtappaṭṭatu.
kuṇavati pōntu araciyaik kāṇṭal
114.	ivvakai moḻivōṉ ṟaṉṉai yiṟaiyavaṉ tēvi mēvum
	cevviyuṅ kuṟiyuñ ceppic ceṉṟava ḷeyta lōṭum
	kavviya kāmat tīyāṟ kayaṅkiya mālai yoppāḷ
	navvinēr viḻiyāy naṉṟō navilkaniṉ karuma meṉṟāḷ.
urai:- ivvakai moḻivōṉ taṉṉai - ivvāṟu colli makiḻum aṭṭapaṅkaṟku, iṟaiyavaṉ tēvi - aracaṉ maṉaiviyākiya amutamati, mēvum cevviyum kuṟiyum ceppi - varakkūṭiya kālamum iṭamum uraittuviṭṭu, ceṉṟavaḷ - tēvipāṟ ceṉṟa kuṇavati, eytalōṭum - amutamatiyai nōkki yaṇukavarutalum, kavviya kāmattīyāl - paṟṟi varuttum kāmattīyiṉāl, kayaṅkiya mālai oppāḷ - cāmpiya pūmālaiyai yoppavaḷāṉa amirtamati; navvinēr viḻiyāy - māṉpōlum kaṇkaḷaiyuṭaiyāy, niṉkarumam - nī mēṟkoṇṭu ceṉṟa kāriyam, naṉṟō - nalantantatō, navilka - colluka, eṉṟāḷ - eṉṟu kēṭṭāḷ e-ṟu.
cevvi- kālam. kuṟi - kuṟikkum iṭam. “ceppi” yeṉṟataṉāl, avaṟṟai yavaṉ viṉaviṉamai peṟṟām. yāṉaikkūṭattiṉ nīṅki amirtamatiyirunta antappurattai yaṭaintāḷeṉṟaṟku, “ceṉṟavaḷ eytalōṭum” eṉṟum, kuṇavati mēṟkoṇṭu ceṉṟa kāriyam muṭiyumō muṭiyātō eṉṟa aiyam oru puṭaiyalaippa, avaṉpāṟ ceṉṟa kāmavēṭkai orupuṭaiyalaippa uṇarvum uṭampum vāṭiyiruttaliṉ, “kavviya kāmattīyāl kayaṅkiya mālai yoppāḷ” eṉṟār. tītō eṉṉāmai, ataṉaiyavaḷ virumpāmaiyālām.
kuṇavati nikaḻntatu kūṟakkēṭṭu makiḻtal
115.	paṭṭatu nalaṅkā ṇeṉpāḷ pākaṉaik kaṇṭa vāṟum
	aṭṭamā paṅkaṉ cīṟi yaḻaṉṟiṭṭa vāṟun tēvi
	iṭṭattiṟ kavaṉai yāṅkē yiyaivitta vāṟuṅ kūṭaṟ
	koṭṭiya kuṟiyuñ coṉṉā ḷoḷḷiḻai yuvappuṟ ṟāḷē.
urai:- nalampaṭṭatu eṉpāḷ - nalamākavē muṭintatu eṉṟu collit toṭaṅkiya tōḻi, pākaṉaik kaṇṭa vāṟum - tāṉ ceṉṟu avyāṉaippākaṉaik kaṇṭatum, aṭṭapaṅkaṉ cīṟi aḻaṉṟiṭṭavāṟum - aṭṭapaṅkaṉeṉṉum peyariṉaṉākiya avaṉ cīṟic ciṉantatum, tēvi  iṭṭattiṟku - aracamātēviyākiya amir tamatiyiṉ viruppattukku,  avaṉai iyaivittavāṟum - avaṉai yuṭaṉpaṭuvittatum, kūṭaṟku oṭṭiya kuṟiyum - avaṉaic ceṉṟu kūṭutaṟku niyamitta kālamum iṭamum, coṉṉāḷ - kuṇavati kūṟiṉāḷ, oḷḷiḻai - oḷḷiya iḻaiyaṇinta amutamati, uvap puṟṟāḷ - makiḻcci mikuntāḷ e-ṟu.
“naṉṟō navilka niṉkarumam” eṉṟu viṉaviṉāṭku nīṭṭiyātu viṭaiyiṟuppāḷ, “paṭṭatu nalam” eṉṟāḷ; kāṇ-acainilai. mā-acai. aḻalutal-vekuḷutal. iṭṭam-vaṭacoṟṟiripu. āṅku eṉpatu uraiyacai. kuṟiyiṉait tāṉē kuṟittāḷātaliṉ, aṭṭa paṅkaṟkēyaṉṟi amirtamatikkum tōḻi kūṟiṉāḷ. uṟṟāḷ- uṟu eṉṉum uriccollaṭiyākap piṟanta muṟṟuviṉai.
amirtamati aracaṉpāl muṉivu koṇṭoḻukal
116.	taṉivayi ṉikuḷai yāṉai tarappaṭu cāra ṉōṭu
	kaṉipurai kiḷavi kāmaṅ kalantaṉaḷ kaṉintu ceṉṟāḷ
	muṉiviṉai maṉṉaṉ ṟaṉmēṉ muṟukiṉa ḷoḻuku muṉpōl
	iṉiyava ḷalla ḷeṉko leṉamaṉat teṇṇi ṉāṉē.
urai:- kaṉipurai kiḷavi - kaṉipōlum coṟkaḷai yuṭaiya amutamati, ikuḷai - tōḻiyākiya kuṇavatipāl, tarappaṭu yāṉaic cāraṉōṭu - kūṭṭappaṭṭa yāṉaippākaṉākiya aṭṭa paṅkaṉuṭaṉ, taṉivayiṉ - taṉiyāṉa ōriṭattē, kāmaṅkaṉintu - kāmavēṭkai ciṟantu, kalantaṉaḷ ceṉṟāḷ - puṇarntuṭaṉoḻuki varupavaḷ, maṉṉaṉ taṉmēl - maṉṉaṉākiya yacōtaraṉpāl, muṉiviṉai muṟukiṉaḷ oḻukum - veṟuppumikak koṇṭoḻu kaluṟṟāḷāka, muṉpōl iṉiyavaḷ allaḷ eṉkol eṉa - muṉpōl ivaḷ nampāl iṉpuṟukiṉṟāḷillai itaṟkukkāraṇam yātākum eṉṟu, maṉattu eṇṇiṉāṉ - yacōtaraṉ taṉmaṉattuk kuḷḷē cintikkalāṉāṉ e-ṟu.
taṉivayiṉ, tōḻi kuṟitta kuṟiyiṭam. ikuḷaitarappaṭuyāṉaic cāraṉōṭu taṉivayiṉ kāmaṅ kaṉintu kalantaṉaḷ ceṉṟāḷ eṉa iyaittukkoḷka. kāmaṅkalantaṉaḷ kaṉintu ceṉṟāḷ eṉṟē koṇṭu kāmappuṇarcci peṟṟu ataṉkaṭkaṉṟi yoḻukupavaḷ eṉṟuraippiṉumām. cāraṉpāl maṉatteḻunta aṉpu ceṉṟamaiyiṉ, aracaṉpāl veṟuppu mikuvāḷāyiṉāḷeṉa vaṟika. ammikuti aracaṉuḷattē aiyattai yeḻuppiṉamaiyiṉ, “muṉpōlaṉpila ḷeṉkol” eṉṟāṉ; itaṉaip piṟar  pāl kūṟi yārāyātu taṉmaṉattakattē yeṇṇi ārāyat toṭaṅkiṉā ṉeṉpār, “maṉatteṇṇiṉāṉ” eṉṟār.
maṉṉaṉ poyyuṟakkam koṇṭu amutamatiyiṉ kaḷavoḻukkiṉaik kāṇṭal
117.	aracavai viṭuttu meyyā laṟuciṉa ṉoppa maṉṉaṉ
	uraiyala ṉamaḷi taṉmē luṟaṅkutal purinta pōḻtiṉ
	viraikamaḻ kuḻali mēvi meyttuyi leṉṟu kāmat
	tuṟaiyiṉaḷ peyarntu tōḻi kuṟiyiṭan tuṉṉi ṉāḷē.
urai:- avai aracu viṭuttu - aracavaikkaṇ vantirunta aracarkaḷait tattam irukkaikkuc cellaviṭuttu, meyyāl  aṟuciṉaṉ oppa - puṟattē maṉattuṭ ciṟitum ciṉam illāṉ pōlak kāṭṭi, uraiyalaṉ - yāvarōṭum ētum collāṭaliṉṟi, amaḷi taṉmēl - taṉṉuṭaiya paṭukkaiyiṉmēl kiṭantu, maṉṉaṉ uṟaṅkutal purinta pōḻtu - maṉṉaṉākiya yacōtaraṉ poyyuṟakkaṅ koṇṭiruk kaiyil, viraikamaḻ kuḻali - maṇam kamaḻum kūntalai yuṭaiya amirtamati, mēvi - avaṉarukē pōntu, meyttuyil eṉṟu - meyyākavē uṟaṅkukiṉṟāṉ eṉṟu karutik koṇṭu, kāmat tuṟaiyiṉaḷ - kāmaviṉ pantuykkum kaḷavu neṟiyiṉaḷāy, peyarntu - paḷḷiyaṟaiyiṉiṉṟum nīṅki, tōḻi kuṟiiṭam tuṉṉiṉāḷ - tōḻi kuṟitta kuṟiyiṭattai yaṭaintāḷ e-ṟu.
amirtamatiyiṉ oḻukalāṟṟiṟ piṟanta aiyam nāḷukkunāḷ mikuntu yacōtaraṉ neñcai yalaittamaiyiṉ, aracavaikkaṇ iruntu ceyyum muṟaimaikkaṇ karuttuc cellāmaiyiṉ, “aracavai viṭuttu” eṉṟum, viṭṭāṉāyiṉum, piṟar ayirāmaip poruṭṭu vekuḷiyaip puṟattē kāṭṭātoḻukiṉa ṉeṉṟaṟku, “meyyālaṟu ciṉaṉoppa”  eṉṟum kūṟiṉār. aṟuciṉaṉ eṉpatu, “aruṅkēṭaṉ”1 eṉṟāṟ pōlvatu. poyyuṟakkaṅ koṇṭā ṉeṉpār. uṟaṅkiṉā ṉeṉṉātu, “uṟaṅkutal purinta pōḻtu” eṉṟār; “purinta” eṉṟataṉāl, kāṇpavar meyyākavē uṟaṅku kiṉṟāṉeṉa uṇarat tōṟṟiṉā ṉeṉpatu peṟṟām. maṇaṅkamaḻum kuḻaliyātaliṉ, amirtamati vantu tāṉ uṟaṅkutalaik kaṇṭeḻu vataik kaṇṇālaṉṟi maṉattā laṟintukoṇṭamai tōṉṟa, “viraikamaḻ kuḻali” yeṉṟār. kaḷavuk kāmat tuṟaiyil oḻukukiṉ ṟāḷātaliṉ, uṭaṉē avviṭat tiṉiṉṟum nīṅkuvatu tōṉṟa, “peyarntu” eṉṟum, “kuṟiyiṭam tuṉṉiṉā” ḷeṉṟum kūṟiṉār.
yacōtaraṉ avaḷaip piṉtoṭarntu ceṉṟu
akkuṟiyiṭattē maṟaintu niṟṟal
118.	tuyiliṉai yoruvi maṉṉaṉ cuṭarkkatir vāḷkai yēnti
	mayaluḻai* vaḻiccel kiṉṟa vāḷari yēṟu pōlak
	kayalviḻi yavaḷtaṉ piṉṉē karantaṉa ṉotuṅki yāṅkaṇ
	ceyaliṉai yaṟitu meṉṟu ceṟintaṉaṉ maṟaintu niṉṟāṉ.
urai:- maṉṉaṉ - amirtamati cellakkaṇṭa yacōtaraṉ, tuyiliṉai yoruvi - tāṉ koṇṭirunta poyyuṟakkattai viṭuttu, cuṭarkkatirvāḷ - cuṭarukiṉṟa oḷikkatirkaḷai vīcum vāṭpaṭaiyai, kaiyēnti - kaiyil ēntik koṇṭu, mayal uḻaivaḻi - mayaṅkiyōṭu kiṉṟa māṉoṉṟaip piṉpaṟṟi, celkiṉṟa vāḷ ariēṟu pōla - cellumoru ciṅkavēṟu pōla, kayalviḻiyavaḷtaṉ piṉṉē - kayalmīṉ pōlum kaṇkaḷaiyuṭaiya amirtamatiyiṉ piṉṉē, karantaṉaṉ otuṅki - maṟaintu ceṉṟu, āṅkaṇ - akkuṟiyiṭattē, ceyaliṉaiyaṟitum eṉṟu - nikaḻum nikaḻcciyaikkāṇpām eṉṟu niṉaittukkoṇṭu, ceṟintaṉaṉ maṟaintu niṉṟāṉ - yārum aṟiyāvakaiyil oṭuṅki maṟaintu niṉṟu nōkkiṉāṉ e-ṟu.
nikaḻcciyiṉaik kāṇṭal vēṇṭu meṉṟē poyyuṟakkaṅ koṇṭāṉātaliṉ, amutamati cellakkaṇṭatum ataṉai yoru viṉāṉ eṉavaṟika. uḻai-māṉ. kāmat tuṟaiyiṉaḷeṉṟā rākaliṉ, ataṟkēṟpa, avaṭku mayaṅkiya māṉai uvamam kūṟiṉār. vāḷ ariyēṟupōla eṉṟu uvamaiyāṟṟalāl yacōtaraṉ mikka ciṉattuṭaṉ celkiṉṟamai peṟṟām. piṟar kāṇpareṉṟa accattāl aṭikkaṭi cuṟṟumuṟṟum parakka viḻittup pārttukkoṇṭē ceṉṟā ḷeṉṟaṟku, amirtamatiyaik “kayal viḻiyavaḷ” eṉṟār. taṉṉiruppup piṟarkkut teriyāmal, piṟar mellap pēcuvatum tāṉ naṉku kēṭkakkūṭiyatāyuḷḷa iṭattaiyaṭainta maṟain tiruntāṉātaliṉ, “ceṟintaṉaṉ” eṉṟār.
amirtamati tāḻttuvarakkaṇṭa aṭṭapaṅkaṉ 
avaḷait tuṉpuṟuttal
119.	kaṭaiyaṉak kamalap pāvai karuṅkuḻal paṟṟik kaiyāl
	iṭainilañ cella īrttiṭ ṭirukaiyi ṉālu mōccip
	puṭaipala puṭaittut tāḻtta poruḷitu pukalka eṉṉāt
	tuṭiyiṭai tuvaḷa vīḻttu nilattiṭait tukaittiṭ ṭāṉē.
